  "description": "Extract titles and metadata from workloads board"
}

- **Workloads board** (konecta-ix-workloads #1): IQ Portal infrastructure migration (Cloud Run -> GKE) - Terraform, Helm, ArgoCD, CI/CD, testing

2. **Requirements items** (#68, #74, #78, #84) inform what the workloads infrastructure needs to support

4. **UAT testing** (apps #44, #96-100) correlates with workloads e2e testing (#81)

I now have a thorough understanding of the codebase. Here is my Phase 1 Surface Discovery analysis.

---

## Phase 1 Surface Discovery Results

### Project Context Summary

**Existing Repo Structure**: The `kix-workload-konectaiq` repository is a **workload infrastructure repo** (not an application code repo). It contains per-environment directories (`dev`, `qa`, `prod`) each with Terraform, Helm, and ArgoCD configuration. The application code lives in a separate repo: `konecta-ix-applications/kix-apps-konectaiq`.

**Key Technologies and Patterns Identified**:
- **GCP**: GKE (Autopilot-style), Artifact Registry (`europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images`), Cloud SQL, Secret Manager, Certificate Manager, Cloud Armor WAF
- **Runners**: Self-hosted `static-ip-runner` (not `ubuntu-latest`)
- **Terraform**: Version 1.7.4, GCS backend, per-environment state buckets
- **Helm**: Per-environment charts with auto-generated `values-{env}.yaml` from `config.json`
- **ArgoCD**: Automated sync with self-heal, deployed from workload repo Helm path
- **Security scanning**: Trivy configured for Terraform IaC scanning (severity MEDIUM)

**Critical Finding -- Shared Workflows Already Exist**: The repo is **already calling** reusable workflows from `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` at `@main`. Three reusable workflows are referenced:
1. `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml`
2. `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml`
3. `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-apply.yml`
4. `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml`

This means the **central repo already exists** (`konecta-ix-services/.github-private`) and the **calling pattern** (reusable workflows via `uses:`) is established. The task is about **expanding** the shared workflow catalog, not creating it from scratch.

**GitHub Org Structure Observed**:
- `konecta-ix-workloads` -- workload repos (this repo)
- `konecta-ix-applications` -- application code repos
- `konecta-ix-services` -- services/shared infrastructure (where `.github-private` lives)
- `konecta-ix-platform` -- platform modules and docs

**Branch/Environment Model**:
- `develop` branch deploys to `dev` only
- `main` branch deploys to `dev` and `qa`
- Version tags (`v*`) deploy to `prod` only
- Production has change windows (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
- Environment approval gates exist (`{env}-approval`)

---

### Task Completeness Assessment

**COMPLETE -- Areas with sufficient detail**:
- Central repo location is effectively answered: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` already exists and is in use
- Calling convention is answered: reusable workflows via `uses:` / `workflow_call` (already in production use)
- Environment model is clear: dev/qa/prod with branch-based gating
- Runner infrastructure is known: `static-ip-runner`
- Artifact Registry location and naming conventions are documented in `config.json`
- Trivy scanning approach is partially established (IaC scanning with severity MEDIUM)

**GAPS IDENTIFIED -- Specific missing business logic**:

1. **Scope of "new" shared workflows**: The task says "create shared/reusable workflows" but four already exist. The real question is: what **additional** workflows need to be created? The GitHub issues (#43, #44, #45, #46) suggest Docker build+push, Helm lint+deploy, OIDC auth, and image promotion -- but the task description does not clearly differentiate between what exists and what needs to be built.

2. **Application code CI workflow design (Issue #43)**: The application code repo (`kix-apps-konectaiq`) is a Node.js/TypeScript/Nuxt app. A "lint, test, build, push" workflow needs clarity on:
   - What linter (ESLint? Biome?), what test runner (Vitest? Jest? Playwright?), what build tool (Nuxt build? Docker multi-stage?)
   - Whether the shared workflow should be language-agnostic or Node-specific
   - How Dockerfile location and build args are parameterized

3. **OIDC/WIF authentication ownership (Issues #8, #45)**: The existing workflows use `id-token: write` permissions and reference environment-based auth, but it is unclear whether OIDC/WIF authentication is handled **inside the shared reusable workflows** or by the **caller workflows**. This is a fundamental architectural question: does the shared `terraform-plan.yml` already handle GCP authentication internally, or does the caller set it up? We cannot see inside `.github-private` from this repo.

4. **Image promotion workflow semantics (Issue #46)**: The task mentions "image promotion (dev to QA to prod)" but the current architecture uses ArgoCD with automated sync pulling from the same Artifact Registry image. How does "promotion" actually work -- is it re-tagging images in Artifact Registry? Updating Helm values with a new tag? Triggering ArgoCD sync? This is undefined.

5. **Cross-org workflow sharing**: The `.github-private` repo is in `konecta-ix-services`, but the calling repos span `konecta-ix-workloads` and `konecta-ix-applications`. Are these orgs configured with workflow sharing permissions? This is a GitHub org-level setting that gates whether cross-org `workflow_call` works at all.

6. **Helm workflow for workload repos (Issue #44, marked "In review")**: Issue #44 suggests a Helm lint+deploy workflow exists or is in progress. What is its current status? Does it already exist in `.github-private`? This directly affects scope.

**CRITICAL UNCERTAINTIES -- Must-have information**:

- We cannot inspect the contents of `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` from this repo. Without knowing what the four existing reusable workflows accept as inputs and outputs, we risk duplicating logic or creating incompatible interfaces.
- The OIDC/WIF Terraform configuration (Issue #8) is listed as "Backlog" -- meaning the infrastructure for keyless auth may not yet exist. Do the current shared workflows use service account keys? If so, the OIDC migration is a prerequisite, not just a workflow concern.

---

### Recommended User Clarifications

#### Priority 1: Critical Scope and Architecture

1. **Question**: What is the current state of the `.github-private` repo? Can you share its directory listing or the interfaces (inputs/secrets/outputs) of the four existing reusable workflows (`terraform-validate.yml`, `terraform-plan.yml`, `terraform-apply.yml`, `argocd-deploy.yml`)?
   - **Impact**: Without knowing what already exists and its interface contracts, we risk creating duplicate or incompatible shared workflows. This is the single most important gap -- the task is to expand a catalog we cannot currently inspect.

2. **Question**: Which of these shared workflows are **net new** vs. modifications of existing ones?
   - **Options**:
     - A) Only new workflows: Docker build+push, Helm lint, image promotion, OIDC auth setup
     - B) New workflows plus refactoring/enhancing the existing Terraform and ArgoCD workflows
     - C) Complete rewrite of all shared workflows including the existing four
   - **Impact**: Determines whether this is an additive task or a refactor. The answer changes effort estimates and risk profile significantly.

3. **Question**: How should OIDC/WIF authentication be structured in the shared workflow architecture?
   - **Options**:
     - A) Each shared reusable workflow handles its own GCP authentication internally (caller just passes WIF provider/service account as inputs)
     - B) A separate shared "authenticate" composite action is called first, then downstream workflows use the established credentials
     - C) Caller workflows handle all authentication; shared workflows assume credentials are already available
   - **Impact**: This is a cross-cutting concern that affects every shared workflow's interface design. Option A makes shared workflows self-contained but requires WIF inputs on every call. Option C keeps shared workflows simple but pushes complexity to callers.

4. **Question**: For the application CI workflow (Issue #43 -- "lint, test, build, push"), should the shared workflow be:
   - **Options**:
     - A) A single monolithic reusable workflow with toggleable stages (lint, test, build, push as boolean inputs)
     - B) Separate reusable workflows per stage (one for lint, one for test, one for Docker build+push) that callers compose
     - C) A composite action for Docker build+push only, with lint/test left to caller-specific workflows
   - **Impact**: Determines reusability vs. flexibility tradeoff. Option A is simpler for callers but harder to customize. Option B is the most flexible but requires callers to wire together multiple workflow_call jobs.

#### Priority 2: Integration and Flow Details

5. **Question**: How should image promotion (Issue #46) work in practice?
   - **Options**:
     - A) Re-tag the Docker image in Artifact Registry (e.g., copy `sha-abc123` from dev to qa tag)
     - B) Update the Helm `values-{env}.yaml` image tag in the workload repo via a PR (ArgoCD auto-syncs)
     - C) A combination: re-tag the image AND update the workload repo Helm values
     - D) Something else (e.g., ArgoCD image updater watches for new tags)
   - **Impact**: This determines whether promotion is an Artifact Registry operation, a Git commit operation, or both. The current `values-dev.yaml` has `tag: "latest"` which suggests the promotion model is not yet finalized.

6. **Question**: Is the Terraform WIF infrastructure (Issue #8) a prerequisite for this task, or should the shared workflows be designed to support both service account key auth (current?) and OIDC auth (future)?
   - **Impact**: If OIDC is not yet provisioned, shared workflows need to work with whatever auth mechanism exists today while being forward-compatible with OIDC.

7. **Question**: Are there other workload repos (besides `kix-workload-konectaiq`) or application repos that will consume these shared workflows? If so, do they follow the same directory structure (`environments/{env}/terraform`, `environments/{env}/helm`, etc.)?
   - **Impact**: Determines how generic the shared workflow inputs need to be. If all consumers follow the same structure, we can make assumptions. If not, the parameterization needs to be more flexible.

---

### Recommendation

- **DECISION**: REQUEST CLARIFICATIONS before proceeding to Phase 2.
- **REASONING**: The most critical gap is that we cannot see inside the existing `.github-private` repository. Four reusable workflows are already in production use, and the task is to expand this catalog. Without knowing the existing workflow interfaces, input contracts, and authentication patterns, we would be designing in the dark. Additionally, the relationship between Issues #8 (WIF Terraform), #45 (OIDC auth), and the shared workflow auth model is ambiguous and architecturally significant.
- **NEXT STEPS**: 
  1. Get access to or a listing of `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` to understand existing workflow interfaces
  2. Clarify questions 1-3 above (scope, existing state, auth model) as they are blockers
  3. Questions 4-7 can be answered in parallel or deferred to early Phase 2

### Codebase Context for Phase 2

Once clarifications are received, these are the key reference points for deep research:

- **Existing caller workflows**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/.github/workflows/` -- all four files demonstrate the calling conventions, input patterns, and orchestration logic
- **Shared workflow references**: The `uses:` lines at `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/{terraform-validate,terraform-plan,terraform-apply,argocd-deploy}.yml@main`
- **Environment config pattern**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/environments/dev/config.json` -- the `global.artifacts` block defines registry/project/repository naming
- **Artifact Registry convention**: `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/{image-name}:{tag}`
- **Trivy configuration**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/trivy.yaml` -- current IaC scanning config
- **Branch/environment gating logic**: The `check-branch` job pattern repeated across all caller workflows (duplicated -- potential candidate for shared workflow extraction)
- **Application tech stack**: Node.js, Express, Nuxt, TypeScript (from `catalog-info.yaml` tags)
- **Runner**: `static-ip-runner` (self-hosted, not GitHub-hosted)
- **Related repos**: `konecta-ix-applications/kix-apps-konectaiq` (app code), `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules` (Terraform modules), `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs` (docs)

# Comprehensive Codebase Analysis Report

## 1. Project Overview

### 1.1 Repository Organization Structure

The platform uses a multi-organization GitHub Enterprise model:

| Organization | Purpose | Shared Workflows Repo |
|---|---|---|
| `konecta-ix-services` | Platform Services (Kong, LiteLLM, Backstage, etc.) | `.github-private` (THIS REPO - contains existing reusable workflows) |
| `konecta-ix-applications` | Containerised application code repos | `.github-private` (planned - for shared CI pipelines) |
| `konecta-ix-workloads` | Workload repos (Helm, ArgoCD, Terraform per app) | `.github-private` (planned - for shared deploy workflows) |
| `konecta-ix-platform` | Platform Engineering (infrastructure, modules, docs, images) | N/A |

The `.github-private` repo at `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private` currently contains **4 reusable workflows** and **0 composite actions**.

### 1.2 Current .github-private File Tree

```
.github-private/
  .github/
    workflows/
      argocd-deploy.yml           # Reusable ArgoCD deployment
      terraform-apply.yml         # Reusable Terraform apply
      terraform-plan.yml          # Reusable Terraform plan
      terraform-validate.yml      # Reusable Terraform validate
  CLAUDE_ORG.md                   # Organization-level standards doc
  profile/
    README.md                     # Organization profile README
```

No composite actions directory exists. No Docker/build/Helm/image-related workflows exist.

### 1.3 Consuming Workload Repo Structure

The workload repo at `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq` has:
```
.github/workflows/
  argocd-deploy.yml              # Calls shared argocd-deploy.yml
  manual-validate-plan.yml       # Calls shared validate + plan
  validate-and-plan.yml          # Auto PR validation, calls shared validate + plan
  validate-plan-apply.yml        # Full deploy pipeline, calls shared validate + plan + apply
environments/
  dev/   config.json, argocd/, helm/, terraform/
  qa/    config.json, argocd/, helm/, terraform/
  prod/  config.json, argocd/, helm/, terraform/
scripts/
  generate-helm-values.sh
trivy.yaml
catalog-info.yaml
```

### 1.4 Key Technologies

- **Terraform** v1.5+ (v1.7.4 used in workflows)
- **Helm** v3.x
- **ArgoCD** v2.13.3
- **GKE** (Google Kubernetes Engine) in `europe-west1`
- **GCP Artifact Registry**: `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images`
- **GCP Workload Identity Federation** (OIDC, keyless auth)
- **Trivy** v0.33.1 (for IaC scanning)
- **Cosign** (for image signing)
- **Application stack**: Node.js, Express, Nuxt, TypeScript (per `catalog-info.yaml` tags)

## 2. Pattern Analysis

### 2.1 Existing Reusable Workflow Interface Contracts

#### 2.1.1 `terraform-validate.yml` (Lines 1-276)
**File**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml`

```yaml
name: z_Terraform Validate (Reusable)
on:
  workflow_call:
    inputs:
      working_directory:     # required, string
      terraform_version:     # optional, string, default '1.7.4'
      enable_trivy:          # optional, boolean, default true
      trivy_severity:        # optional, string, default 'MEDIUM'
    secrets:
      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID:      # optional
      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:  # optional
    outputs:
      validation_status:     # 'success' or 'failure'
      trivy_findings_count:  # integer
      trivy_critical_count:  # integer
      trivy_high_count:      # integer
```

**Key patterns**:
- Name prefix `z_` (sorts to bottom in GitHub UI)
- Runs on `static-ip-runner` (custom self-hosted runner)
- No GCP auth (does `terraform init -backend=false`)
- Uses `trivy-config: 'trivy.yaml'` from the calling repo
- Uploads SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning + artifacts
- Generates `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with severity breakdown table

#### 2.1.2 `terraform-plan.yml` (Lines 1-309)
**File**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml`

```yaml
name: z_Terraform Plan (Reusable)
on:
  workflow_call:
    inputs:
      working_directory:     # required, string
      terraform_version:     # optional, string, default '1.7.4'
      environment:           # required, string (dev, qa, prod)
      comment_on_pr:         # optional, boolean, default true
      upload_artifact:       # optional, boolean, default false
    secrets:
      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID:      # optional
      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:  # optional
    outputs:
      plan_status:           # 'success', 'failure', or 'no_changes'
      changes_detected:      # 'true'/'false'
      add_count:             # integer
      change_count:          # integer
      destroy_count:         # integer
```

**Key patterns**:
- Self-contained GCP auth using `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` and `vars.SERVICE_NAME` from calling repo's GitHub Environment variables
- WIF pool ID convention: `github-${SERVICE_NAME}-${ENV}`
- WIF provider: `projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider`
- Service account: `${POOL_ID}@${GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com`
- Optional `GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID` override variable
- Uses `google-github-actions/auth@v2` for OIDC
- Uses `google-github-actions/setup-gcloud@v2` for Cloud SDK
- Uses `actions/create-github-app-token@v1` for private modules
- PR comments via `actions/github-script@v7`
- Plan statistics uploaded as artifacts for downstream consumption

#### 2.1.3 `terraform-apply.yml` (Lines 1-246)
**File**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-apply.yml`

```yaml
name: z_Terraform Apply (Reusable)
on:
  workflow_call:
    inputs:
      working_directory:     # required, string
      terraform_version:     # optional, string, default '1.7.4'
      environment:           # required, string
      require_approval:      # optional, boolean, default true
    secrets:
      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID:      # optional
      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:  # optional
    outputs:
      apply_status:          # 'success' or 'failure'
      tf_outputs:            # JSON string of terraform outputs
      deployment_time:       # ISO 8601 timestamp
```

**Key patterns**:
- Uses `environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}` for GitHub Environment protection rules
- Same GCP auth block as terraform-plan (duplicated pattern)
- Fresh plan before apply (drift detection)
- Skip apply if no changes detected
- Post-apply validation with `terraform plan -detailed-exitcode`
- Artifact upload (apply output + outputs.json) with 90-day retention

#### 2.1.4 `argocd-deploy.yml` (Lines 1-161)
**File**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml`

```yaml
name: z_ArgoCD Deploy (Reusable)
on:
  workflow_call:
    inputs:
      application_file:      # required, string (path to ArgoCD YAML)
      argocd_version:        # optional, string, default '2.13.3'
      wait_for_sync:         # optional, boolean, default true
      sync_timeout:          # optional, number, default 300
    secrets:
      argocd_auth_token:     # required
    outputs:
      app_name:              # deployed application name
      sync_status:           # final sync status
```

**Key patterns**:
- NO GCP auth (uses ArgoCD auth token directly)
- ArgoCD server: `argocd-ci.ix.konecta-digital.com`
- `--grpc-web` flag for ArgoCD CLI
- Installs ArgoCD CLI from GitHub releases (multi-arch support)
- Uses `argocd app create -f "$FILE" --upsert` for idempotent deploys
- Waits for in-flight operations, syncs, then waits for healthy

### 2.2 GCP Auth Pattern (Self-Contained OIDC)

The critical auth pattern from existing shared workflows (terraform-plan.yml lines 82-116):

```bash
# Read from calling repo's environment variables
PROJECT_NUMBER="${{ vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER }}"
SERVICE_NAME="${{ vars.SERVICE_NAME }}"
ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
POOL_ID="github-${SERVICE_NAME}-${ENV}"
PROVIDER="projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider"

# Allow explicit GKE project ID override
GKE_PROJECT="${{ vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID }}"
if [ -z "$GKE_PROJECT" ]; then
  GKE_PROJECT="kd-ix-eur-${ENV}-gke"
fi
SA="${POOL_ID}@${GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
```

This pattern uses:
- `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` (GitHub Environment variable on the calling repo)
- `vars.SERVICE_NAME` (GitHub Environment variable on the calling repo)
- Constructs WIF pool/provider/SA dynamically
- Each shared workflow handles its own auth (self-contained)

### 2.3 Naming Conventions

**Workflow file names**:
- Shared workflows: `z_<name>.yml` prefix (sorts alphabetically after consuming workflows)
- Consumer trigger workflows: kebab-case, no prefix (e.g., `validate-and-plan.yml`)

**Workflow display names**:
- Shared: `z_Terraform Validate (Reusable)` / `z_ArgoCD Deploy (Reusable)`
- Consumer trigger: `Auto Validate and Plan` / `.Validate, Plan and Apply` (dot prefix sorts first)
- Consumer manual: `.Validate and Plan` (dot prefix for manual-first ordering)

**GitHub references**:
- Shared workflow calls: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/<name>.yml@main`

### 2.4 Runner Configuration

All workflows use `runs-on: static-ip-runner` (custom self-hosted runner). The crewai old workflows used `runs-on: ubuntu-latest`.

### 2.5 Branch-to-Environment Gating

The consuming repo implements this gating pattern (consistent across all consumer workflows):

```
develop -> dev only
main    -> dev, qa
v* tags -> prod only
```

This is implemented in a `check-branch` job at the start of each consumer workflow. It is NOT in the shared workflows -- the gating logic lives in each consuming repo.

### 2.6 Concurrency Controls

```yaml
concurrency:
  group: deploy-${{ inputs.environment }}
  cancel-in-progress: false  # Never cancel in-progress deployments
```

PR workflows use:
```yaml
concurrency:
  group: pr-${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
  cancel-in-progress: true   # Cancel superseded PR checks
```

## 3. Artifact Registry & Image Conventions

### 3.1 Registry Structure

From `config.json` (all three environments share):
```json
"artifacts": {
  "registry": "europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev",
  "project": "kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts",
  "repository": "approved-images"
}
```

**Full image path**: `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/<image-name>:<tag>`

The `kix-platform-images` repo (`/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-images/`) manages the approved images supply chain by mirroring upstream images. Application-built images would also go to this registry.

### 3.2 Image Naming from config.json

```json
"image": {
  "name": "kix-app-iqportal",
  "tag": "latest"
}
```

The `generate-helm-values.sh` script constructs the full path:
```bash
IMAGE_REPO="${ar_registry}/${ar_project}/${ar_repository}/${image_name}"
# => europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/kix-app-iqportal
```

### 3.3 Docker Build Pattern (from crewai old workflows)

**File**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/container-security.yaml`

Key patterns observed:
- Uses `docker/setup-buildx-action@v3`
- Uses `docker/build-push-action@v6` with `cache-from: type=gha` and `cache-to: type=gha,mode=max`
- Enables `provenance: true` and `sbom: true`
- Tags with both `${{ github.sha }}` and `latest`
- After push, waits 60s for Google Container Analysis auto-scan
- Uses `gcloud artifacts docker images describe` to get vulnerability counts
- Fails on CRITICAL or HIGH vulnerabilities
- Cosign keyless signing after build+scan passes

### 3.4 Image Promotion Pattern (from crewai old workflows)

**File**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/promote-release.yaml`

Current pattern updates Helm values files via `yq` and creates a PR. For the workload repo pattern, promotion would need to:
1. Re-tag the image in Artifact Registry (source env tag -> target env tag)
2. Update `values-{env}.yaml` with the new tag (or update `config.json` and regenerate)

## 4. Environment Configuration

### 4.1 config.json Schema

Each environment has a `config.json` at `environments/{env}/config.json`. The schema has two top-level keys:

- `global`: Shared infrastructure references (GKE cluster, project IDs, network CIDRs, artifacts registry, Cloud SQL, WAF, IAP, labels)
- `service`: Application-specific config (name, namespace, SA, hostname, certs, database, secrets, env vars, image, gateway, network settings)

**Key fields for new workflows**:
- `global.artifacts.registry` = `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev`
- `global.artifacts.project` = `kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts`
- `global.artifacts.repository` = `approved-images`
- `service.image.name` = `kix-app-iqportal`
- `service.image.tag` = `latest` (this is what promotion would update)
- `service.hostname` = e.g., `iq-portal.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com`

### 4.2 Environment Differences

| Field | dev | qa | prod |
|-------|-----|-----|------|
| `global.environment` | `dev` | `qa` | `prod` |
| `global.domain_base` | `dev.ix.konecta-digital.com` | `qa.ix.konecta-digital.com` | `ix.konecta-digital.com` |
| `service.namespace` | `dev-iqportal` | `qa-iqportal` | `prod-iqportal` |
| `service.env.log_level` | `debug` | `info` | `info` |
| ArgoCD sync | automated (prune + selfHeal) | manual | manual |

### 6.1 Currently EXISTS in `.github-private`

Additionally needed:

### 6.3 Workflow Design Constraints

Based on the existing patterns, new workflows MUST:

1. Use `name: z_<Name> (Reusable)` prefix convention
2. Use `on: workflow_call:` trigger
3. Run on `static-ip-runner` (or provide input to override)
4. Handle GCP auth internally using `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` + `vars.SERVICE_NAME` pattern
5. Provide `outputs:` for downstream job consumption
6. Generate `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with formatted tables
7. Upload artifacts where appropriate
8. Use `permissions:` block (least privilege)
9. Use `continue-on-error: true` for scan steps that should report but not block

### 6.4 Application Code Repo Pattern

The application code repo is at `konecta-ix-applications/kix-apps-konectaiq` (per catalog-info.yaml annotation `github.com/project-slug: konecta-ix-applications/kix-apps-konectaiq`).

From the PRP document and catalog-info.yaml:
- **Dockerfile** exists with: port 8080, health check `/health`, user `nodejs:1001`
- **Tech stack**: Node.js, Express, Nuxt, TypeScript
- **Application code org**: `konecta-ix-applications`
- **Workload infra org**: `konecta-ix-workloads`

The master CLAUDE.md (line 76-79) shows `konecta-ix-applications` has its own `.github-private` for shared CI pipelines. The new Docker build workflow likely belongs in `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` (shared across both service AND application repos) OR in a separate `konecta-ix-applications/.github-private`.

## 7. Validation Strategy

### 7.1 Available Validation Commands

**Terraform**:
```bash
terraform init -backend=false
terraform validate
terraform fmt -check -recursive
```

**Helm**:
```bash
helm lint environments/{env}/helm/kix-app-iqportal/ \
  -f environments/{env}/helm/kix-app-iqportal/values.yaml \
  -f environments/{env}/helm/kix-app-iqportal/values-{env}.yaml

helm template test environments/{env}/helm/kix-app-iqportal/ \
  -f environments/{env}/helm/kix-app-iqportal/values.yaml \
  -f environments/{env}/helm/kix-app-iqportal/values-{env}.yaml
```

**Values generation**:
```bash
./scripts/generate-helm-values.sh {env} --check
```

**Trivy IaC**:
```bash
trivy config --config trivy.yaml environments/{env}/terraform/
```

**Trivy container** (new):
```bash
trivy image --severity CRITICAL,HIGH <image:tag>
```

### 7.2 GitHub Actions YAML Validation

Workflows can be validated with:
```bash
python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('<file>'))"
```

Or with `actionlint` if available.

---

## 8. Development Guidelines

### 8.1 Shared Workflow Conventions

1. **File location**: `.github/workflows/` in the `.github-private` repo
2. **Naming**: `z_<kebab-case-name>.yml` (the `z_` prefix is used for all 4 existing workflows)
3. **Display name**: `z_<Title Case Name> (Reusable)`
4. **Trigger**: `on: workflow_call:` only
5. **Runner**: `static-ip-runner` (consistent across all existing workflows)
6. **Permissions**: Explicitly declared, least privilege
7. **Error handling**: `continue-on-error: true` for non-blocking steps; `if: always()` for summaries
8. **Summaries**: Always generate `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with markdown tables
9. **Artifacts**: Upload with `actions/upload-artifact@v4`, sensible retention (5-90 days)
10. **Checkout**: Always use `actions/checkout@v4` as first step

### 8.2 Consumer Workflow Conventions

1. **File location**: `.github/workflows/` in each consuming repo
2. **Display name**: Prefixed with `.` for manual triggers (sorts first in UI)
3. **Branch gating**: Implemented in consumer, not in shared workflow
4. **Pre-flight checks**: Verify files exist before calling shared workflows
5. **Concurrency**: Environment-scoped groups, `cancel-in-progress: false` for deploys
6. **Production guards**: Change window enforcement (Tue-Thu 20:00-23:00 UTC)
7. **Summary job**: Always present, aggregates results from all upstream jobs

### 8.3 Secrets and Variables Model

**Secrets** (per-repo or per-org):
- `ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN`: ArgoCD authentication
- `TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID` / `TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY`: Private Terraform modules access

**GitHub Environment Variables** (per-environment in each consuming repo):
- `GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER`: GCP project number for WIF
- `SERVICE_NAME`: Service identifier for WIF pool naming
- `GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID`: Optional override for GKE project

### 8.4 Image Tag Strategy

Current pattern uses `latest` tag in all environments. For promotion, the expected flow is:
1. App CI builds image tagged with `sha` and pushes to `approved-images` repository
2. Image promotion re-tags from source env tag to target env tag
3. `config.json` is updated with new image tag
4. `generate-helm-values.sh` regenerates `values-{env}.yaml`
5. ArgoCD detects the change and syncs

### 8.5 Key Integration Points

- **ArgoCD server**: `argocd-ci.ix.konecta-digital.com` (grpc-web)
- **Artifact Registry**: `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images`
- **GCS state buckets**: `kd-tfstate-services-{env}`
- **WIF naming**: `github-{SERVICE_NAME}-{ENV}` pool + `github-provider` provider
- **GKE projects**: `kd-ix-eur-{env}-gke` (default)
- **External Secrets Operator**: ClusterSecretStore named `gcp-secret-manager`

### 8.6 Multi-Org Consideration

The master CLAUDE.md reveals that `konecta-ix-applications` also has a `.github-private` for "Shared CI pipeline" and `konecta-ix-workloads` also plans one for "Shared deploy wkfl". The new workflows being designed here go into `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` but could be called by repos in ANY of these organizations, since GitHub allows cross-org workflow references.

Current calling convention:
```yaml
uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/<workflow>.yml@main
```

    49→## Goal
    50→
    51→Produce a complete, production-ready workload repository that enables GitOps deployment of the IQ Portal application to dev, QA, and prod GKE environments via ArgoCD, with Terraform-managed infrastructure (namespaces, IAM, secrets, database, certificates) and Helm-managed Kubernetes workloads (deployment, services, autoscaling, networking).
    52→
    53→## Why
    54→
    55→- **Business value**: P0 pilot — validates the entire kix-infrastructure platform migration path for ~24 services
    56→- **Integration**: Establishes the pattern that all subsequent workload repos will follow
    57→- **Problems solved**: Moves from Cloud Run (per-request billing, vendor lock-in) to standardized GKE (GitOps, observability, security controls)
    58→
    59→## What
    60→
    61→A workload repository containing:
    62→1. **Three environment configurations** (dev/qa/prod) each with `config.json`, Terraform, Helm, and ArgoCD
    63→2. **Terraform** creating: namespace, GCP SA, Workload Identity, Cloud SQL database + user, Secret Manager secrets, ExternalSecrets, Certificate Manager resources, IAM cross-project bindings
    64→3. **Helm chart** deploying: Express.js app container + Cloud SQL Auth Proxy sidecar, ClusterIP service, HPA, PDB, network policies, GKE Gateway, HTTPRoutes, GCPBackendPolicy, HealthCheckPolicy
    65→4. **ArgoCD** manifests for automated GitOps sync per environment
    66→5. **GitHub Actions** workflows for Terraform validate/plan/apply and ArgoCD deployment
    67→6. **Supporting files**: generate-helm-values.sh, catalog-info.yaml, CODEOWNERS, .gitignore, trivy.yaml

     1→name: z_Terraform Plan (Reusable)
     2→
     3→# Reusable workflow for running Terraform plan in service repositories
     4→# Supports private Terraform modules via GitHub App authentication
     5→on:
     6→  workflow_call:
     7→    inputs:
     8→      working_directory:
     9→        description: 'Terraform directory to plan'
    10→        required: true
    11→        type: string
    12→      terraform_version:
    13→        description: 'Terraform version to use'
    14→        required: false
    15→        type: string
    16→        default: '1.7.4'
    17→      environment:
    18→        description: 'Environment name (dev, qa, prod)'
    19→        required: true
    20→        type: string
    21→      comment_on_pr:
    22→        description: 'Post plan summary as PR comment'
    23→        required: false
    24→        type: boolean
    25→        default: true
    26→      upload_artifact:
    27→        description: 'Upload plan file as artifact'
    28→        required: false
    29→        type: boolean
    30→        default: false
    31→    secrets:
    32→      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID:
    33→        description: 'GitHub App ID for private module access'
    34→        required: false
    35→      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:
    36→        description: 'GitHub App private key for private module access'
    37→        required: false
    38→    outputs:
    39→      plan_status:
    40→        description: 'Plan status (success, failure, or no_changes)'
    41→        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.status }}
    42→      changes_detected:
    43→        description: 'Whether changes were detected (true/false)'
    44→        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.changes_detected }}
    45→      add_count:
    46→        description: 'Number of resources to add'
    47→        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.add_count }}
    48→      change_count:
    49→        description: 'Number of resources to change'
    50→        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.change_count }}
    51→      destroy_count:
    52→        description: 'Number of resources to destroy'
    53→        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.destroy_count }}
    54→
    55→permissions:
    56→  id-token: write
    57→  contents: read
    58→  pull-requests: write
    59→
    60→jobs:
    61→  plan:
    62→    name: Terraform Plan
    63→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    64→    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
    65→    outputs:
    66→      status: ${{ steps.plan-result.outputs.status }}
    67→      changes_detected: ${{ steps.plan-result.outputs.changes_detected }}
    68→      add_count: ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.add_count }}
    69→      change_count: ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.change_count }}
    70→      destroy_count: ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.destroy_count }}
    71→
    72→    steps:
    73→      - name: Checkout code
    74→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    75→
    76→      - name: Setup Terraform
    77→        uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3
    78→        with:
    79→          terraform_version: ${{ inputs.terraform_version }}
    80→          terraform_wrapper: false
    81→
    82→      - name: Configure GCP Auth
    83→        id: gcp-auth-config
    84→        run: |
    85→          # Read from calling repo's environment variables
    86→          PROJECT_NUMBER="${{ vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER }}"
    87→          SERVICE_NAME="${{ vars.SERVICE_NAME }}"
    88→
    89→          if [ -z "$PROJECT_NUMBER" ] || [ -z "$SERVICE_NAME" ]; then
    90→            echo "::error::Missing required environment variables."
    91→            echo "::error::Set GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER and SERVICE_NAME as GitHub Environment variables for '${{ inputs.environment }}'."
    92→            exit 1
    93→          fi
    94→
    95→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
    96→          POOL_ID="github-${SERVICE_NAME}-${ENV}"
    97→          PROVIDER="projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider"
    98→
    99→          # Allow explicit GKE project ID override (e.g., shared env uses shr abbreviation)
   100→          GKE_PROJECT="${{ vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID }}"
   101→          if [ -z "$GKE_PROJECT" ]; then
   102→            GKE_PROJECT="kd-ix-eur-${ENV}-gke"
   103→          fi
   104→          SA="${POOL_ID}@${GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
   105→
   106→          echo "provider_id=${PROVIDER}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   107→          echo "service_account=${SA}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   108→          echo "Using WIF pool: ${POOL_ID} (project ${PROJECT_NUMBER})"
   109→          echo "Service account: ${SA}"
   110→
   111→      - name: Authenticate to GCP
   112→        uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2
   113→        with:
   114→          workload_identity_provider: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.provider_id }}
   115→          service_account: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.service_account }}
   116→
   117→      - name: Setup Cloud SDK
   118→        uses: google-github-actions/setup-gcloud@v2
   119→
   120→      - name: Generate GitHub App Token
   121→        id: app-token
   122→        uses: actions/create-github-app-token@v1
   123→        continue-on-error: true
   124→        with:
   125→          app-id: ${{ secrets.TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID }}
   126→          private-key: ${{ secrets.TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
   127→          owner: konecta-ix-platform
   128→
   129→      - name: Configure Git for Private Modules
   130→        if: steps.app-token.outcome == 'success'
   131→        run: |
   132→          git config --global url."https://x-access-token:${{ steps.app-token.outputs.token }}@github.com/".insteadOf "https://github.com/"
   133→          echo "Configured git for private module access"
   134→
   135→      - name: Terraform Init
   136→        run: terraform init
   137→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   138→
   139→      - name: Terraform Plan
   140→        id: plan
   141→        run: |
   142→          terraform plan -out=tfplan -no-color 2>&1 | tee plan_output.txt
   143→          EXIT_CODE=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
   144→          echo "exit_code=$EXIT_CODE" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   145→          exit $EXIT_CODE
   146→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   147→        continue-on-error: true
   148→
   149→      - name: Terraform Show
   150→        id: show
   151→        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
   152→        run: |
   153→          terraform show -no-color tfplan > plan_readable.txt
   154→          head -n 50 plan_readable.txt > plan_summary.txt
   155→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   156→
   157→      - name: Check for changes
   158→        id: check-changes
   159→        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
   160→        run: |
   161→          if grep -q "No changes" plan_readable.txt; then
   162→            echo "changes_detected=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   163→          else
   164→            echo "changes_detected=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   165→          fi
   166→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   167→
   168→      - name: Extract plan statistics
   169→        id: extract-stats
   170→        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
   171→        run: |
   172→          ADD_COUNT=$(grep -oP 'Plan: \K\d+(?= to add)' plan_output.txt || echo "0")
   173→          CHANGE_COUNT=$(grep -oP '\K\d+(?= to change)' plan_output.txt || echo "0")
   174→          DESTROY_COUNT=$(grep -oP '\K\d+(?= to destroy)' plan_output.txt || echo "0")
   175→
   176→          cat > plan_stats_${{ inputs.environment }}.json <<EOF
   177→          {
   178→            "directory": "${{ inputs.working_directory }}",
   179→            "environment": "${{ inputs.environment }}",
   180→            "status": "success",
   181→            "add": ${ADD_COUNT},
   182→            "change": ${CHANGE_COUNT},
   183→            "destroy": ${DESTROY_COUNT}
   184→          }
   185→          EOF
   186→
   187→          echo "add_count=${ADD_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   188→          echo "change_count=${CHANGE_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   189→          echo "destroy_count=${DESTROY_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   190→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   191→
   192→      - name: Create failure statistics
   193→        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'failure'
   194→        run: |
   195→          cat > plan_stats_${{ inputs.environment }}.json <<EOF
   196→          {
   197→            "directory": "${{ inputs.working_directory }}",
   198→            "environment": "${{ inputs.environment }}",
   199→            "status": "failure",
   200→            "add": 0,
   201→            "change": 0,
   202→            "destroy": 0
   203→          }
   204→          EOF
   205→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   206→
   207→      - name: Upload plan statistics
   208→        if: always()
   209→        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
   210→        with:
   211→          name: plan-stats-${{ inputs.environment }}-${{ hashFiles(inputs.working_directory) }}
   212→          path: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}/plan_stats_${{ inputs.environment }}.json
   213→          retention-days: 7
   214→
   215→      - name: Comment on PR
   216→        if: inputs.comment_on_pr && github.event_name == 'pull_request' && steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
   217→        uses: actions/github-script@v7
   218→        with:
   219→          script: |
   220→            const fs = require('fs');
   221→            const planSummary = fs.readFileSync('${{ inputs.working_directory }}/plan_summary.txt', 'utf8');
   222→            const planOutput = fs.readFileSync('${{ inputs.working_directory }}/plan_output.txt', 'utf8');
   223→
   224→            const addMatch = planOutput.match(/Plan: (\d+) to add/);
   225→            const changeMatch = planOutput.match(/(\d+) to change/);
   226→            const destroyMatch = planOutput.match(/(\d+) to destroy/);
   227→
   228→            const toAdd = addMatch ? addMatch[1] : '0';
   229→            const toChange = changeMatch ? changeMatch[1] : '0';
   230→            const toDestroy = destroyMatch ? destroyMatch[1] : '0';
   231→
   232→            const body = `## Terraform Plan: \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`
   233→
   234→            **Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}
   235→            **Changes:** ${toAdd} to add, ${toChange} to change, ${toDestroy} to destroy
   236→
   237→            <details>
   238→            <summary>Show Plan</summary>
   239→
   240→            \`\`\`terraform
   241→            ${planSummary}
   242→            \`\`\`
   243→
   244→            </details>
   245→
   246→            *Plan generated at: ${new Date().toISOString()}*
   247→            `;
   248→
   249→            github.rest.issues.createComment({
   250→              issue_number: context.issue.number,
   251→              owner: context.repo.owner,
   252→              repo: context.repo.repo,
   253→              body: body
   254→            });
   255→
   256→      - name: Upload plan artifact
   257→        id: upload-plan
   258→        if: inputs.upload_artifact && steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
   259→        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
   260→        with:
   261→          name: tfplan-${{ inputs.environment }}-${{ hashFiles(inputs.working_directory) }}
   262→          path: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}/tfplan
   263→          retention-days: 30
   264→
   265→      - name: Plan Result
   266→        id: plan-result
   267→        if: always()
   268→        run: |
   269→          if [ "${{ steps.plan.outcome }}" == "failure" ]; then
   270→            echo "status=failure" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   271→            echo "changes_detected=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   272→            exit 1
   273→          elif [ "${{ steps.check-changes.outputs.changes_detected }}" == "false" ]; then
   274→            echo "status=no_changes" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   275→            echo "changes_detected=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   276→          else
   277→            echo "status=success" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   278→            echo "changes_detected=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   279→          fi
   280→
   281→      - name: Summary
   282→        if: always()
   283→        run: |
   284→          echo "## [${{ inputs.environment }}] Terraform Plan" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   285→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   286→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   287→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   288→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   289→
   290→          if [ "${{ steps.plan.outcome }}" == "success" ]; then
   291→            if [ "${{ steps.check-changes.outputs.changes_detected }}" == "true" ]; then
   292→              echo "**Plan Status:** Changes detected" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   293→              echo "- **Changes:** ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.add_count }} to add, ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.change_count }} to change, ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.destroy_count }} to destroy" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   294→              echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   295→              echo "<details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   296→              echo "<summary>Plan Output (first 50 lines)</summary>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   297→              echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   298→              echo "\`\`\`terraform" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   299→              sed '' plan_summary.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY || cat plan_summary.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   300→              echo "\`\`\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   301→              echo "</details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   302→            else
   303→              echo "**Plan Status:** No changes" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   304→            fi
   305→          else
   306→            echo "**Plan Status:** Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   307→          fi
   308→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   309→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

     1→name: z_Terraform Validate (Reusable)
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  workflow_call:
     5→    inputs:
     6→      working_directory:
     7→        description: 'Terraform directory to validate'
     8→        required: true
     9→        type: string
    10→      terraform_version:
    11→        description: 'Terraform version to use'
    12→        required: false
    13→        type: string
    14→        default: '1.7.4'
    15→      enable_trivy:
    16→        description: 'Enable Trivy security scanning'
    17→        required: false
    18→        type: boolean
    19→        default: true
    20→      trivy_severity:
    21→        description: 'Minimum severity for Trivy findings (CRITICAL, HIGH, MEDIUM, LOW)'
    22→        required: false
    23→        type: string
    24→        default: 'MEDIUM'
    25→    secrets:
    26→      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID:
    27→        description: 'GitHub App ID for private module access'
    28→        required: false
    29→      TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:
    30→        description: 'GitHub App private key for private module access'
    31→        required: false
    32→    outputs:
    33→      validation_status:
    34→        description: 'Validation status (success or failure)'
    35→        value: ${{ jobs.validate.outputs.status }}
    36→      trivy_findings_count:
    37→        description: 'Number of Trivy security findings'
    38→        value: ${{ jobs.validate.outputs.findings_count }}
    39→      trivy_critical_count:
    40→        description: 'Number of CRITICAL severity findings'
    41→        value: ${{ jobs.validate.outputs.critical_count }}
    42→      trivy_high_count:
    43→        description: 'Number of HIGH severity findings'
    44→        value: ${{ jobs.validate.outputs.high_count }}
    45→
    46→jobs:
    47→  validate:
    48→    name: Validate Terraform
    49→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    50→    outputs:
    51→      status: ${{ steps.validation-result.outputs.status }}
    52→      findings_count: ${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.findings_count }}
    53→      critical_count: ${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.critical_count }}
    54→      high_count: ${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.high_count }}
    55→
    56→    steps:
    57→      - name: Checkout code
    58→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    59→
    60→      - name: Setup Terraform
    61→        uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3
    62→        with:
    63→          terraform_version: ${{ inputs.terraform_version }}
    64→
    65→      - name: Terraform Format Check
    66→        id: fmt
    67→        run: terraform fmt -recursive -check
    68→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
    69→        continue-on-error: true
    70→
    71→      - name: Format Check Result
    72→        if: steps.fmt.outcome == 'failure'
    73→        run: |
    74→          echo "::error::Terraform format check failed in ${{ inputs.working_directory }}"
    75→          echo "::error::Run 'terraform fmt -recursive' in ${{ inputs.working_directory }} to fix formatting"
    76→          echo "::error::Validation pipeline stopped - Init and Validate steps were not executed"
    77→          exit 1
    78→
    79→      - name: Generate GitHub App Token
    80→        id: app-token
    81→        uses: actions/create-github-app-token@v1
    82→        continue-on-error: true
    83→        with:
    84→          app-id: ${{ secrets.TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID }}
    85→          private-key: ${{ secrets.TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}
    86→          owner: konecta-ix-platform
    87→
    88→      - name: Configure Git for Private Modules
    89→        if: steps.app-token.outcome == 'success'
    90→        run: |
    91→          git config --global url."https://x-access-token:${{ steps.app-token.outputs.token }}@github.com/".insteadOf "https://github.com/"
    92→          echo "Configured git for private module access"
    93→
    94→      - name: Terraform Init (Backend-less)
    95→        id: init
    96→        run: terraform init -backend=false
    97→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
    98→
    99→      - name: Terraform Validate
   100→        id: validate
   101→        run: terraform validate -no-color
   102→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   103→
   104→      - name: Run Trivy Security Scan (SARIF)
   105→        if: inputs.enable_trivy
   106→        uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1
   107→        with:
   108→          scan-type: 'config'
   109→          scan-ref: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   110→          trivy-config: 'trivy.yaml'
   111→          format: 'sarif'
   112→          output: 'trivy-results.sarif'
   113→          severity: 'LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH,CRITICAL'
   114→          exit-code: '0'
   115→
   116→      - name: Run Trivy Security Scan (Table)
   117→        if: inputs.enable_trivy
   118→        uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1
   119→        continue-on-error: true
   120→        with:
   121→          scan-type: 'config'
   122→          scan-ref: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   123→          trivy-config: 'trivy.yaml'
   124→          format: 'table'
   125→          output: 'trivy-results.txt'
   126→          severity: 'LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH,CRITICAL'
   127→          exit-code: '0'
   128→
   129→      - name: Display Trivy Findings
   130→        if: inputs.enable_trivy && always()
   131→        continue-on-error: true
   132→        run: |
   133→          echo "## Trivy Security Scan Results" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   134→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   135→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   136→          echo "**Scan Scope:** All severities (LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, CRITICAL)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   137→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   138→
   139→          if [ -f trivy-results.txt ]; then
   140→            CRITICAL=$(grep -oP 'CRITICAL:\s*\K\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   141→            HIGH=$(grep -oP 'HIGH:\s*\K\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   142→            MEDIUM=$(grep -oP 'MEDIUM:\s*\K\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   143→            LOW=$(grep -oP 'LOW:\s*\K\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   144→            TOTAL=$((CRITICAL + HIGH + MEDIUM + LOW))
   145→          else
   146→            CRITICAL=0
   147→            HIGH=0
   148→            MEDIUM=0
   149→            LOW=0
   150→            TOTAL=0
   151→          fi
   152→
   153→          echo "### Severity Breakdown" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   154→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   155→          echo "| Severity | Count | Status |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   156→          echo "|----------|-------|--------|" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   157→          echo "| CRITICAL | $CRITICAL | $([ "$CRITICAL" -gt 0 ] && echo "Review Required" || echo "Clean") |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   158→          echo "| HIGH | $HIGH | $([ "$HIGH" -gt 0 ] && echo "Review Required" || echo "Clean") |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   159→          echo "| MEDIUM | $MEDIUM | $([ "$MEDIUM" -gt 0 ] && echo "Informational" || echo "Clean") |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   160→          echo "| LOW | $LOW | $([ "$LOW" -gt 0 ] && echo "Informational" || echo "Clean") |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   161→          echo "| **TOTAL** | **$TOTAL** | |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   162→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   163→
   164→          if [ -f trivy-results.txt ]; then
   165→            echo "### Detailed Scan Report" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   166→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   167→            echo "<details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   168→            echo "<summary>Click to expand full Trivy output</summary>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   169→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   170→            echo "\`\`\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   171→            cat trivy-results.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   172→            echo "\`\`\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   173→            echo "</details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   174→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   175→          fi
   176→
   177→      - name: Upload Trivy SARIF
   178→        if: inputs.enable_trivy && always()
   179→        continue-on-error: true
   180→        uses: github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v4
   181→        with:
   182→          sarif_file: trivy-results.sarif
   183→          category: trivy-${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   184→
   185→      - name: Upload Trivy Results as Artifact
   186→        if: inputs.enable_trivy && always()
   187→        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
   188→        with:
   189→          name: trivy-results-${{ hashFiles(inputs.working_directory) }}
   190→          path: |
   191→            trivy-results.sarif
   192→            trivy-results.txt
   193→          retention-days: 5
   194→
   195→      - name: Count Trivy findings by severity
   196→        id: trivy-counts
   197→        if: inputs.enable_trivy && always()
   198→        run: |
   199→          if [ -f trivy-results.txt ]; then
   200→            CRITICAL=$(grep -oP 'CRITICAL:\s*\K\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   201→            HIGH=$(grep -oP 'HIGH:\s*\K\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')
   202→            TOTAL=$((CRITICAL + HIGH))
   203→
   204→            echo "findings_count=$TOTAL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   205→            echo "critical_count=$CRITICAL" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   206→            echo "high_count=$HIGH" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   207→
   208→            if [ "$CRITICAL" -gt 0 ] || [ "$HIGH" -gt 0 ]; then
   209→              echo "::warning::Found $CRITICAL CRITICAL and $HIGH HIGH severity findings"
   210→            fi
   211→          else
   212→            echo "findings_count=0" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   213→            echo "critical_count=0" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   214→            echo "high_count=0" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   215→          fi
   216→
   217→      - name: Validation Result
   218→        id: validation-result
   219→        if: always()
   220→        run: |
   221→          if [ "${{ steps.fmt.outcome }}" == "failure" ] || \
   222→             [ "${{ steps.init.outcome }}" == "failure" ] || \
   223→             [ "${{ steps.validate.outcome }}" == "failure" ]; then
   224→            echo "status=failure" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   225→            exit 1
   226→          else
   227→            echo "status=success" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   228→          fi
   229→
   230→      - name: Summary
   231→        if: always()
   232→        run: |
   233→          echo "## Terraform Validation Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   234→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   235→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   236→          echo "**Terraform Version:** ${{ inputs.terraform_version }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   237→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   238→
   239→          if [ "${{ steps.fmt.outcome }}" == "failure" ]; then
   240→            echo "## FORMAT CHECK FAILED" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   241→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   242→            echo "**Fix:** Run \`terraform fmt -recursive\` in \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   243→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   244→            echo "---" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   245→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   246→          fi
   247→
   248→          echo "| Check | Status |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   249→          echo "|-------|--------|" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   250→
   251→          if [ "${{ steps.fmt.outcome }}" == "success" ]; then
   252→            echo "| Format | Passed |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   253→          else
   254→            echo "| Format | **FAILED** |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   255→          fi
   256→
   257→          if [ "${{ steps.init.outcome }}" == "success" ]; then
   258→            echo "| Init | Passed |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   259→          elif [ "${{ steps.init.outcome }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   260→            echo "| Init | Skipped (blocked by format failure) |" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   261→          else

   263→          fi

   267→          elif [ "${{ steps.validate.outcome }}" == "skipped" ]; then

   271→          fi

   273→          if [ "${{ inputs.enable_trivy }}" == "true" ]; then

check_status: string      # \"success\" or \"failure\"\n\n# image-promote.yml interface\ninputs:\n  image_name: string        # e.g., \"kix-app-iqportal\"\n  source_tag: string        # source image tag (e.g., SHA from dev build)\n  target_environment: string # \"qa\" or \"prod\"\n  workload_repo: string    # e.g., \"konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-konectaiq\"\n  config_path: string       # path to config.json, e.g., \"environments/qa/config.json\"\n  require_approval: boolean # default: true (uses GitHub Environment protection)\nsecrets:\n  WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID: string          # GitHub terraform-validate.yml (validation + summary format)\n  - Implement: workflow_call trigger with inputs/outputs as defined above\n  - Implement: helm lint with --strict flag and multiple values files\n  - Implement: helm template for render validation\n  - Implement: $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY with lint/template results\n  - NO GCP auth needed (local-only operation)\n\nTask 3:\nCREATE konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-values-check.yml:\n  - MIRROR pattern from: terraform-validate.yml (validation + summary format)\n  - Implement: workflow_call trigger with inputs/outputs as defined above\n  - Implement: Run generate-helm-values.sh with --check flag\n  - Implement: $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY with check result\n  - NO GCP auth needed (local-only operation)\n\nTask 4:\nCREATE konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/image-promote.yml:\n  - MIRROR pattern from: terraform-plan.yml (GCP auth) + terraform-apply.yml (environment approval)\n  - MIRROR pattern from: kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/promote-release.yaml (PR creation)\n  - Implement: workflow_call trigger with inputs/secrets/outputs as defined above\n  - Implement: GCP OIDC auth for Artifact Registry access\n  - Implement: gcloud artifacts docker tags add for image re-tagging\n  - Implement: GitHub App token for cross-repo access\n  - Implement: Clone workload repo, update config.json image tag via jq\n  - Implement: Create PR with peter-evans/create-pull-request@v6 or gh CLI\n  - Implement: $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY with promotion details, PR link\n  - Implement: environment input for approval gates (production promotion requires approval)\n```\n\n### Per-task pseudocode\n\n#### Task 1: docker-build-push.yml\n\n```yaml\n# Pseudocode - key steps only\nname: z_Docker Build Push (Reusable)\non: workflow_call:\n  inputs: { image_name, dockerfile_path, build_context, environment, push, enable_trivy, trivy_severity, enable_cosign, build_args }\n  outputs: { image_uri, image_tag, trivy_findings_count, trivy_critical_count, trivy_high_count, scan_passed }\n\npermissions:\n  id-token: write       # OIDC for GCP + Cosign\n  contents: read\n  security-events: write # SARIF upload\n  packages: write        # Container registry\n\njobs:\n  build-push:\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}\n    steps:\n      # 1. Checkout\n      # 2. Configure GCP Auth (MIRROR terraform-plan.yml lines 82-116)\n      # 3. Authenticate to GCP (google-github-actions/auth@v2)\n      # 4. Configure Docker for AR (gcloud auth configure-docker europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev)\n      # 5. Setup Docker Buildx (docker/setup-buildx-action@v3)\n      # 6. Generate image metadata\n      #    IMAGE_REPO=europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${image_name}\n      #    TAG=${{ github.sha }}\n      #    FULL_IMAGE=${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG}\n      # 7. Build and Push (docker/build-push-action@v6)\n      #    CRITICAL: Use cache-from/cache-to type=gha for layer caching\n      #    CRITICAL: Enable provenance: true, sbom: true\n      #    Tags: ${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG}, ${IMAGE_REPO}:latest\n      # 8. Trivy scan (if enabled)\n      #    aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1 scan-type: image\n      #    SARIF + table output (same dual-format as terraform-validate)\n      #    Upload SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning\n      # 9. Cosign sign (if enabled and scan passed)\n      #    sigstore/cosign-installer@v3\n      #    cosign sign --yes ${IMAGE_REPO}@${DIGEST}\n      #    CRITICAL: --yes flag for keyless/non-interactive\n      # 10. Summary with image URI, tag, scan results, signing status\n```\n\n#### Task 2: helm-lint.yml\n\n```yaml\n# Pseudocode\nname: z_Helm Lint (Reusable)\non: workflow_call:\n  inputs: { chart_path, values_files, strict, environment }\n  outputs: { lint_status, template_status }\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  lint:\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    steps:\n      # 1. Checkout\n      # 2. Setup Helm (azure/setup-helm@v4)\n      # 3. Helm Lint\n      #    Construct -f flags from comma-separated values_files input\n      #    helm lint ${chart_path} -f val1.yaml -f val2.yaml [--strict]\n      # 4. Helm Template\n      #    helm template test-release ${chart_path} -f val1.yaml -f val2.yaml\n      #    Validates rendered output is valid YAML\n      # 5. Summary table: lint pass/fail, template pass/fail\n```\n\n#### Task 3: helm-values-check.yml\n\n```yaml\n# Pseudocode\nname: z_Helm Values Check (Reusable)\non: workflow_call:\n  inputs: { environment, script_path }\n  outputs: { check_status }\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  check:\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    steps:\n      # 1. Checkout\n      # 2. Install jq (dependency of generate-helm-values.sh)\n      # 3. Run script with --check flag\n      #    bash ${script_path} ${environment} --check\n      #    Exit code 0 = values match, non-zero = drift detected\n      # 4. Summary: check result, drift details if any\n```\n\n#### Task 4: image-promote.yml\n\n```yaml\n# Pseudocode\nname: z_Image Promote (Reusable)\non: workflow_call:\n  inputs: { image_name, source_tag, target_environment, workload_repo, config_path, require_approval }\n  secrets: { WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID, WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }\n  outputs: { promoted_image_uri, pr_url, pr_number }\n\npermissions:\n  id-token: write   # GCP OIDC for AR\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  promote:\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    environment: ${{ inputs.require_approval && inputs.target_environment || '' }}\n    # PATTERN: environment field enables GitHub protection rules for approval\n    steps:\n      # 1. Configure GCP Auth (MIRROR terraform-plan.yml)\n      # 2. Authenticate to GCP\n      # 3. Verify source image exists\n      #    gcloud artifacts docker images describe \\\n      #      europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${image_name}:${source_tag}\n      # 4. Add target tag to image\n      #    gcloud artifacts docker tags add \\\n      #      europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${image_name}:${source_tag} \\\n      #      europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${image_name}:${target_environment}-${source_tag}\n      # 5. Generate GitHub App token for workload repo\n      #    actions/create-github-app-token@v1\n      # 6. Clone workload repo\n      #    git clone https://x-access-token:${TOKEN}@github.com/${workload_repo}.git\n      # 7. Update config.json image tag\n      #    jq '.service.image.tag = \"${source_tag}\"' ${config_path} > tmp && mv tmp ${config_path}\n      # 8. Regenerate Helm values (if generate-helm-values.sh exists)\n      #    bash scripts/generate-helm-values.sh ${target_environment}\n      # 9. Create PR via gh CLI or peter-evans/create-pull-request\n      #    Branch: promote/${image_name}/${target_environment}/${source_tag}\n      #    Title: \"promote: ${image_name} to ${target_environment} (${source_tag})\"\n      # 10. Summary: promoted image URI, PR link, approval status\n```\n\n### Integration Points\n\n```yaml\n# Consumer Workflow Integration\nCALLING_CONVENTION:\n  - reference: 'konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/<name>.yml@main'\n  - pattern: Same as existing terraform-* and argocd-deploy workflows\n  - example: |\n      jobs:\n        build:\n          uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml@main\n          with:\n            image_name: kix-app-iqportal\n            environment: dev\n          secrets:\n            # No additional secrets needed — GCP auth uses vars from GitHub Environment\n\nGITHUB_ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLES:\n  - required: vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER (per environment on consuming repo)\n  - required: vars.SERVICE_NAME (per environment on consuming repo)\n  - optional: vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID (override for non-standard project naming)\n\nARTIFACT_REGISTRY:\n  - registry: 'europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev'\n  - project: 'kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts'\n  - repository: 'approved-images'\n  - pattern: '{registry}/{project}/{repository}/{image_name}:{tag}'\n\nOIDC/WIF:\n  - pool_id: 'github-{SERVICE_NAME}-{ENV}'\n  - provider: 'projects/{PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/{POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider'\n  - service_account: '{POOL_ID}@{GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com'\n\nCONFIG_UPDATE:\n  - file: 'environments/{env}/config.json'\n  - field: '.service.image.tag'\n  - regen: './scripts/generate-helm-values.sh {env}'\n```\n\n## Validation Loop\n\n### Level 1: Syntax & Style\n\n```bash\n# Validate YAML syntax for all new workflow files\nfor f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do\n  python3 -c \"import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/$f'))\"\ndone\n\n# Validate workflow naming convention (must start with z_)\nfor f in .github/workflows/*.yml; do\n  NAME=$(python3 -c \"import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('$f'))['name'])\")\n  echo \"$f -> $NAME\"\n  # Verify name starts with z_\ndone\n\n# Expected: No errors. All names match z_<Name> (Reusable) pattern.\n```\n\n## Final Validation Checklist\n\n- [ ] All 4 YAML files pass syntax validation\n- [ ] All workflow names follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention\n- [ ] All workflows use `on: workflow_call:` trigger\n- [ ] All workflows run on `static-ip-runner`\n- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` and `image-promote.yml` implement self-contained GCP OIDC auth matching `terraform-plan.yml` pattern\n- [ ] `helm-lint.yml` and `helm-values-check.yml` do NOT require GCP auth\n- [ ] All workflows define structured `outputs:` for downstream consumption\n- [ ] All workflows generate `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with markdown tables\n- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` uploads Trivy SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning\n- [ ] `image-promote.yml` creates a PR (not a direct commit) for config updates\n- [ ] No hardcoded project IDs, registry paths, or service names — all parameterized via inputs or vars\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n- Don't hardcode `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images` — construct from `vars` or inputs\n- Don't skip Trivy scanning — it's a security gate, not optional\n- Don't use `ubuntu-latest` — all runners must be `static-ip-runner`\n- Don't duplicate the GCP auth block — copy it exactly from `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116\n- Don't commit directly to workload repos — always create a PR for config changes\n- Don't mix `secrets` and `vars` — GCP credentials use `vars` (environment variables), not `secrets`\n- Don't use `docker/login-action` for AR auth — use `gcloud auth configure-docker` after OIDC auth\n- Don't forget `--yes` flag on `cosign sign` — required for non-interactive keyless signing\n\n---\n\n## Task Breakdown\n\nSee [docs/tasks/shared-github-actions-workflows.md](../tasks/shared-github-actions-workflows.md) for detailed implementation tasks.\n"
}

### PRP Analysis Summary

The PRP describes adding 4 new reusable GitHub Actions workflows to `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/` that standardize Docker build/push, Helm validation, and image promotion across ~24 services. Complexity is rated Moderate-Complex due to GCP OIDC/WIF auth, Trivy scanning, Cosign keyless signing, and cross-repo PR creation.

### Task Breakdown (5 Tasks)

| Task ID | Name | Priority | Dependencies |
|---------|------|----------|-------------|
| **T-001** | Create `helm-lint.yml` reusable workflow | High | None |
| **T-002** | Create `helm-values-check.yml` reusable workflow | High | None |
| **T-003** | Create `docker-build-push.yml` reusable workflow | Critical | T-001, T-002 (soft) |
| **T-004** | Create `image-promote.yml` reusable workflow | Critical | T-003 |
| **T-005** | Validate all workflows against conventions | High | T-001-T-004 |

### Sequencing

```
T-001 (helm-lint) ──────────────┐
                                ├──> T-003 (docker-build-push) ──> T-004 (image-promote) ──> T-005 (validation)
T-002 (helm-values-check) ──────┘
```

- **T-001 and T-002** are independent and parallelizable (simplest workflows, no GCP auth)
- **T-003** is the most complex single workflow (GCP OIDC, Docker buildx, Trivy, Cosign)
- **T-004** builds on T-003's GCP auth pattern and adds cross-repo PR creation
- **T-005** validates all 4 workflows against the PRP's final validation checklist and anti-patterns

### Key Implementation Guardrails

Each task includes detailed acceptance criteria in Given-When-Then format, code patterns to mirror (especially `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116 for GCP OIDC auth), and validation scripts. The document also captures all anti-patterns from the PRP (no `ubuntu-latest`, no `docker/login-action`, no hardcoded registry paths, must use `--yes` on `cosign sign`, etc.).

---

## PRP: Shared Reusable GitHub Actions Workflows

**Confidence Score: 8/10**

**Location**: `docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md`

### Scope: 4 New Workflows for `.github-private`

| Workflow | Purpose | GCP Auth |
|---|---|---|
| `docker-build-push.yml` | Build, Trivy scan, Cosign sign, push to AR | Yes (self-contained OIDC) |
| `helm-lint.yml` | Helm lint + template render validation | No |
| `helm-values-check.yml` | Validate generated values match committed | No |
| `image-promote.yml` | Re-tag in AR + PR to update config.json | Yes (self-contained OIDC) |

### Key Design Decisions

- **Auth model**: Each workflow handles GCP OIDC internally using `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` + `vars.SERVICE_NAME` (mirrors `terraform-plan.yml` pattern)
- **Promotion**: Both re-tags in Artifact Registry AND creates a PR to update `config.json` in the workload repo
- **Conventions**: `z_` prefix, `workflow_call` trigger, `static-ip-runner`, structured outputs, `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`
- **No changes** to existing 4 workflows (Terraform validate/plan/apply + ArgoCD deploy)

### Task Breakdown

**Location**: `docs/tasks/shared-github-actions-workflows.md` (5 tasks)

```
T-001 (helm-lint) ────────────┐
                               ├──> T-003 (docker-build-push) ──> T-004 (image-promote) ──> T-005 (validation)
T-002 (helm-values-check) ────┘
```

T-001 and T-002 can be done in parallel (simplest, no GCP auth). T-003 is the most complex (OIDC + buildx + Trivy + Cosign). T-004 adds cross-repo PR creation. T-005 validates all workflows against conventions.
