     1→# PRP: Shared Reusable GitHub Actions Workflows
     2→
     3→**PRP Confidence Score: 8/10** — All four existing workflows available as interface templates, reference implementations exist in crewai/platform-images repos, auth pattern established. -1 for cross-org workflow sharing (needs org admin confirmation), -1 for image promotion flow requiring both AR retag + Git commit across repos.
     4→
     5→---
     6→
     7→## Discovery Summary
     8→
     9→### Initial Task Analysis
    10→
    11→Expand the shared reusable GitHub Actions workflow catalog in `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` with new workflows for Docker image build/push, Helm chart validation, container security scanning, and image promotion across environments (dev/qa/prod). These workflows will be consumed by any workload or application repository across the `konecta-ix-workloads`, `konecta-ix-applications`, and `konecta-ix-services` GitHub organizations.
    12→
    13→### User Clarifications Received
    14→
    15→- **Question**: Where should the shared workflows live?
    16→- **Answer**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` — the repo already exists with 4 Terraform/ArgoCD workflows.
    17→- **Impact**: Additive work only. Follow existing naming conventions (`z_` prefix, `workflow_call` trigger).
    18→
    19→- **Question**: Net new vs. modifications to existing workflows?
    20→- **Answer**: Only add new workflows. Existing Terraform/ArgoCD workflows stay as-is.
    21→- **Impact**: No breaking changes to current consumers. New workflows extend the catalog.
    22→
    23→- **Question**: How should OIDC/WIF authentication work?
    24→- **Answer**: Self-contained per workflow. Each shared workflow handles its own GCP auth internally using `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` + `vars.SERVICE_NAME`.
    25→- **Impact**: New workflows must implement the same auth block from `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116.
    26→
    27→- **Question**: How should image promotion work?
    28→- **Answer**: Both re-tag in Artifact Registry AND update Helm values in the workload repo.
    29→- **Impact**: Promotion workflow needs both `gcloud artifacts docker tags add` and a Git commit (or PR) to update `config.json` / `values-{env}.yaml`.
    30→
    31→### Missing Requirements Identified
    32→
    33→- Cross-org workflow sharing permissions must be enabled at the GitHub org level for `konecta-ix-workloads` and `konecta-ix-applications` to call workflows in `konecta-ix-services` — this is already working for Terraform workflows, so it's confirmed.
    34→- Cosign keyless signing requires the OIDC token issuer to be trusted — already established in platform-images workflows.
    35→- The `generate-helm-values.sh` script reads `config.json` and generates `values-{env}.yaml` — promotion must update `config.json`, not `values-{env}.yaml` directly.
    36→
    37→## Goal
    38→
    39→Add four new reusable workflows to `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` that enable any application or workload repository to:
    40→1. Build, scan, sign, and push Docker images to Artifact Registry
    41→2. Lint and validate Helm charts
    42→3. Validate that generated Helm values match committed values
    43→4. Promote container images across environments (dev → qa → prod) by re-tagging in AR and updating workload repo configuration
    44→
    45→## Why
    46→
    47→- **Business value**: Standardized CI/CD across ~24 services migrating to GKE. Every workload gets production-grade build, scan, sign, and promotion without duplicating workflow logic.
    48→- **Integration**: Extends the existing shared workflow catalog (Terraform validate/plan/apply + ArgoCD deploy) to cover the full application lifecycle.
    49→- **Problems solved**: Eliminates per-repo CI/CD boilerplate, enforces security scanning and image signing consistently, provides a repeatable promotion model for dev→qa→prod.
    50→
    51→## What
    52→
    53→Four new reusable workflows added to `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/`:
    54→
    55→1. **`docker-build-push.yml`** — Multi-stage Docker build, Trivy vulnerability scan, Cosign keyless signing, push to Artifact Registry
    56→2. **`helm-lint.yml`** — Helm lint + template validation for chart correctness
    57→3. **`helm-values-check.yml`** — Validate generated Helm values match committed values using `generate-helm-values.sh --check`
    58→4. **`image-promote.yml`** — Re-tag image in Artifact Registry + create PR to update `config.json` image tag in workload repo
    59→
    60→### Success Criteria
    61→
    62→- [ ] All four workflows pass YAML syntax validation (`python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open(f))"`)
    63→- [ ] Workflows follow existing conventions: `z_` name prefix, `workflow_call` trigger, `static-ip-runner`, structured outputs, `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`
    64→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` accepts image name, Dockerfile path, build context, and environment as inputs
    65→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` handles GCP OIDC auth self-contained using `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` + `vars.SERVICE_NAME`
    66→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` includes Trivy container scan with configurable severity threshold
    67→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` includes Cosign keyless signing
    68→- [ ] `helm-lint.yml` accepts chart path and values files as inputs
    69→- [ ] `helm-values-check.yml` accepts environment and script path as inputs
    70→- [ ] `image-promote.yml` accepts source env, target env, image name, and workload repo as inputs
    71→- [ ] `image-promote.yml` re-tags image in AR and creates PR to update config.json
    72→
    73→## All Needed Context
    74→
    75→### Research Phase Summary
    76→
    77→- **Codebase patterns found**: Complete reference implementation in 4 existing reusable workflows at `.github-private`, plus Docker build patterns in `kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/container-security.yaml` and image promotion in `kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/promote-release.yaml`, plus image syncing in `kix-platform-images/.github/workflows/sync-images.yml`

   149→### Known Gotchas & Platform Quirks
   150→
   151→```yaml
   152→# CRITICAL: All workflows MUST use static-ip-runner, NOT ubuntu-latest
   153→# The self-hosted runner has specific tools pre-installed
   154→
   155→# CRITICAL: GCP auth uses vars (environment variables), not secrets
   156→# vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER and vars.SERVICE_NAME are set per GitHub Environment on each consuming repo
   157→
   158→# CRITICAL: WIF pool naming convention is "github-{SERVICE_NAME}-{ENV}"
   159→# The provider is always "github-provider" within the pool
   160→
   161→# CRITICAL: Artifact Registry is SHARED across environments
   162→# Registry: europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images
   163→# All envs push to same registry/repository, differentiated by image tags
   164→
   165→# CRITICAL: config.json is the source of truth for image tags
   166→# generate-helm-values.sh reads config.json and produces values-{env}.yaml
   167→# Promotion must update config.json, NOT values-{env}.yaml directly
   168→
   169→# CRITICAL: Cross-org workflow calls require the full path
   170→# uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/<name>.yml@main
   171→
   172→# GOTCHA: Trivy action version pinned to 0.33.1 in existing workflows
   173→# Use same version for consistency unless upgrading all
   174→
   175→# GOTCHA: Cosign keyless signing requires id-token: write permission
   176→# The OIDC token from GitHub Actions is used as the identity for signing
   177→
   178→# GOTCHA: gcloud artifacts docker tags add requires Artifact Registry Writer role
   179→# The WIF service account needs roles/artifactregistry.writer
   180→
   181→# GOTCHA: PR creation for config updates needs contents: write + pull-requests: write
   182→# May need a GitHub App token for cross-repo PRs (konecta-ix-services -> konecta-ix-workloads)
   183→```
   184→
   185→## Implementation Blueprint
   186→
   187→### Data Models and Structure
   188→
   189→No database models. The key data structures are the workflow input/output interfaces:
   190→
   191→```yaml
   192→# docker-build-push.yml interface
   193→inputs:
   194→  image_name: string        # e.g., "kix-app-iqportal"
   195→  dockerfile_path: string   # default: "./Dockerfile"
   196→  build_context: string     # default: "."
   197→  environment: string       # "dev", "qa", "prod"
   198→  push: boolean             # default: true
   199→  enable_trivy: boolean     # default: true
   200→  trivy_severity: string    # default: "CRITICAL,HIGH"
   201→  enable_cosign: boolean    # default: true
   202→  build_args: string        # optional, newline-separated KEY=VALUE
   203→outputs:
   204→  image_uri: string         # full image URI with digest
   205→  image_tag: string         # the SHA-based tag
   206→  trivy_findings_count: string
   207→  trivy_critical_count: string
   208→  trivy_high_count: string
   209→  scan_passed: string       # "true"/"false"
   210→
   211→# helm-lint.yml interface
   212→inputs:
   213→  chart_path: string        # e.g., "environments/dev/helm/kix-app-iqportal"
   214→  values_files: string      # comma-separated, e.g., "values.yaml,values-dev.yaml"
   215→  strict: boolean           # default: true (--strict flag)
   216→  environment: string       # for labeling in summaries
   217→outputs:
   218→  lint_status: string       # "success" or "failure"
   219→  template_status: string   # "success" or "failure"
   220→
   221→# helm-values-check.yml interface
   222→inputs:
   223→  environment: string       # "dev", "qa", "prod"
   224→  script_path: string       # default: "./scripts/generate-helm-values.sh"
   225→outputs:
   226→  check_status: string      # "success" or "failure"
   227→
   228→# image-promote.yml interface
   229→inputs:
   230→  image_name: string        # e.g., "kix-app-iqportal"
   231→  source_tag: string        # source image tag (e.g., SHA from dev build)
   232→  target_environment: string # "qa" or "prod"
   233→  workload_repo: string    # e.g., "konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-konectaiq"
   234→  config_path: string       # path to config.json, e.g., "environments/qa/config.json"
   235→  require_approval: boolean # default: true (uses GitHub Environment protection)
   236→secrets:
   237→  WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID: string          # GitHub App for cross-repo PR
   238→  WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY: string # GitHub App private key
   239→outputs:
   240→  promoted_image_uri: string  # full image URI with new tag
   241→  pr_url: string              # URL of created PR
   242→  pr_number: string           # PR number
   243→```

   292→### Per-task pseudocode
   293→
   294→#### Task 1: docker-build-push.yml
   295→
   296→```yaml
   297→# Pseudocode - key steps only
   298→name: z_Docker Build Push (Reusable)
   299→on: workflow_call:
   300→  inputs: { image_name, dockerfile_path, build_context, environment, push, enable_trivy, trivy_severity, enable_cosign, build_args }
   301→  outputs: { image_uri, image_tag, trivy_findings_count, trivy_critical_count, trivy_high_count, scan_passed }
   302→
   303→permissions:
   304→  id-token: write       # OIDC for GCP + Cosign
   305→  contents: read
   306→  security-events: write # SARIF upload
   307→  packages: write        # Container registry
   308→
   309→jobs:
   310→  build-push:
   311→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
   312→    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
   313→    steps:
   314→      # 1. Checkout
   315→      # 2. Configure GCP Auth (MIRROR terraform-plan.yml lines 82-116)
   316→      # 3. Authenticate to GCP (google-github-actions/auth@v2)
   317→      # 4. Configure Docker for AR (gcloud auth configure-docker europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev)
   318→      # 5. Setup Docker Buildx (docker/setup-buildx-action@v3)
   319→      # 6. Generate image metadata
   320→      #    IMAGE_REPO=europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${image_name}
   321→      #    TAG=${{ github.sha }}
   322→      #    FULL_IMAGE=${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG}
   323→      # 7. Build and Push (docker/build-push-action@v6)
   324→      #    CRITICAL: Use cache-from/cache-to type=gha for layer caching
   325→      #    CRITICAL: Enable provenance: true, sbom: true
   326→      #    Tags: ${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG}, ${IMAGE_REPO}:latest
   327→      # 8. Trivy scan (if enabled)
   328→      #    aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1 scan-type: image
   329→      #    SARIF + table output (same dual-format as terraform-validate)
   330→      #    Upload SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning
   331→      # 9. Cosign sign (if enabled and scan passed)
   332→      #    sigstore/cosign-installer@v3
   333→      #    cosign sign --yes ${IMAGE_REPO}@${DIGEST}
   334→      #    CRITICAL: --yes flag for keyless/non-interactive
   335→      # 10. Summary with image URI, tag, scan results, signing status
   336→```

   432→### Integration Points
   433→
   434→```yaml
   435→# Consumer Workflow Integration
   436→CALLING_CONVENTION:
   437→  - reference: 'konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/<name>.yml@main'
   438→  - pattern: Same as existing terraform-* and argocd-deploy workflows
   439→  - example: |
   440→      jobs:
   441→        build:
   442→          uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml@main
   443→          with:
   444→            image_name: kix-app-iqportal
   445→            environment: dev
   446→          secrets:
   447→            # No additional secrets needed — GCP auth uses vars from GitHub Environment
   448→
   449→GITHUB_ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLES:
   450→  - required: vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER (per environment on consuming repo)
   451→  - required: vars.SERVICE_NAME (per environment on consuming repo)
   452→  - optional: vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID (override for non-standard project naming)
   453→
   454→ARTIFACT_REGISTRY:
   455→  - registry: 'europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev'
   456→  - project: 'kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts'
   457→  - repository: 'approved-images'
   458→  - pattern: '{registry}/{project}/{repository}/{image_name}:{tag}'
   459→
   460→OIDC/WIF:
   461→  - pool_id: 'github-{SERVICE_NAME}-{ENV}'
   462→  - provider: 'projects/{PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/{POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider'
   463→  - service_account: '{POOL_ID}@{GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com'
   464→
   465→CONFIG_UPDATE:
   466→  - file: 'environments/{env}/config.json'
   467→  - field: '.service.image.tag'
   468→  - regen: './scripts/generate-helm-values.sh {env}'
   469→```
   470→
   471→## Validation Loop
   472→
   473→### Level 1: Syntax & Style
   474→
   475→```bash
   476→# Validate YAML syntax for all new workflow files
   477→for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   478→  python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/$f'))"
   479→done
   480→
   481→# Validate workflow naming convention (must start with z_)
   482→for f in .github/workflows/*.yml; do
   483→  NAME=$(python3 -c "import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('$f'))['name'])")
   484→  echo "$f -> $NAME"
   485→  # Verify name starts with z_
   486→done
   487→
   488→# Expected: No errors. All names match z_<Name> (Reusable) pattern.
   489→```
   490→
   491→## Final Validation Checklist
   492→
   493→- [ ] All 4 YAML files pass syntax validation
   494→- [ ] All workflow names follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention
   495→- [ ] All workflows use `on: workflow_call:` trigger
   496→- [ ] All workflows run on `static-ip-runner`
   497→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` and `image-promote.yml` implement self-contained GCP OIDC auth matching `terraform-plan.yml` pattern
   498→- [ ] `helm-lint.yml` and `helm-values-check.yml` do NOT require GCP auth
   499→- [ ] All workflows define structured `outputs:` for downstream consumption
   500→- [ ] All workflows generate `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with markdown tables
   501→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` uploads Trivy SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning
   502→- [ ] `image-promote.yml` creates a PR (not a direct commit) for config updates
   503→- [ ] No hardcoded project IDs, registry paths, or service names — all parameterized via inputs or vars
   504→
   505→---
   506→
   507→## Anti-Patterns to Avoid
   508→
   509→- Don't hardcode `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images` — construct from `vars` or inputs
   510→- Don't skip Trivy scanning — it's a security gate, not optional
   511→- Don't use `ubuntu-latest` — all runners must be `static-ip-runner`
   512→- Don't duplicate the GCP auth block — copy it exactly from `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116
   513→- Don't commit directly to workload repos — always create a PR for config changes
   514→- Don't mix `secrets` and `vars` — GCP credentials use `vars` (environment variables), not `secrets`
   515→- Don't use `docker/login-action` for AR auth — use `gcloud auth configure-docker` after OIDC auth
   516→- Don't forget `--yes` flag on `cosign sign` — required for non-interactive keyless signing
   517→
   518→---

    59→
    60→### T-001: Create Helm Lint Reusable Workflow
    61→
    62→**Task ID**: T-001
    63→**Task Name**: Create `helm-lint.yml` reusable workflow for Helm chart validation
    64→**Priority**: High
    65→
    66→#### Context & Background
    67→
    68→**Source PRP Document**: [docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md](/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md) -- Task 2
    69→
    70→**Feature Overview**: This is one of four new reusable GitHub Actions workflows being added to the shared workflow catalog in `konecta-ix-services/.github-private`. It provides standardized Helm chart linting and template validation that any workload or application repository can call.
    71→
    72→**Task Purpose**:
    73→- **As a** workload repository CI/CD pipeline
    74→- **I need** a reusable workflow that lints and validates Helm charts
    75→- **So that** chart correctness is enforced consistently across all ~24 services without duplicating workflow logic
    76→
    77→#### Dependencies
    78→- **Prerequisite Tasks**: None (simplest workflow, no GCP auth needed)
    79→- **Parallel Tasks**: T-002 (helm-values-check.yml)
    80→- **Integration Points**: Consumer repos call via `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-lint.yml@main`
    81→- **Blocked By**: None
    82→
    83→#### Technical Requirements
    84→
    85→**Functional Requirements**:
    86→- **REQ-1**: When called via `workflow_call`, the workflow shall accept `chart_path`, `values_files`, `strict`, and `environment` as inputs
    87→- **REQ-2**: The workflow shall run `helm lint` on the specified chart path with all provided values files
    88→- **REQ-3**: When `strict` is true, the workflow shall pass the `--strict` flag to `helm lint`
    89→- **REQ-4**: The workflow shall run `helm template` to validate that rendered output is valid YAML
    90→- **REQ-5**: The workflow shall output `lint_status` and `template_status` as structured outputs
    91→- **REQ-6**: The workflow shall generate a `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with markdown table showing lint and template results
    92→
    93→**Non-Functional Requirements**:
    94→- **Runner**: Must use `static-ip-runner`, NOT `ubuntu-latest`
    95→- **Naming**: Workflow `name:` must follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention
    96→- **Permissions**: Only `contents: read` required (no GCP auth)
    97→
    98→#### Implementation Details
    99→
   100→**Files to Create**:
   101→```
   102→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-lint.yml - Helm lint + template reusable workflow
   103→```
   104→
   105→**Key Implementation Steps**:
   106→1. **Define workflow_call interface** -- inputs: `chart_path` (string, required), `values_files` (string, default: "values.yaml"), `strict` (boolean, default: true), `environment` (string, default: "dev"); outputs: `lint_status`, `template_status`
   107→2. **Set permissions** -- `contents: read` only
   108→3. **Create `lint` job** on `static-ip-runner`:
   109→   - Checkout with `actions/checkout@v4`
   110→   - Setup Helm with `azure/setup-helm@v4`
   111→   - Parse comma-separated `values_files` input into `-f` flags
   112→   - Run `helm lint $chart_path -f val1.yaml -f val2.yaml [--strict]`
   113→   - Run `helm template test-release $chart_path -f val1.yaml -f val2.yaml`
   114→   - Set output values based on exit codes
   115→4. **Generate step summary** -- Markdown table with lint pass/fail, template pass/fail, chart path, environment label
   116→
   117→**Code Patterns to Follow**:
   118→- **Workflow structure**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml` -- validation + summary format
   119→- **Naming convention**: All existing workflows use `z_` prefix (e.g., `z_Terraform Validate (Reusable)`)
   120→- **Output pattern**: Use `$GITHUB_OUTPUT` for structured outputs consumed by caller
   121→- **Summary format**: Follow markdown table format from `terraform-validate.yml` step summary
   122→
   123→#### Acceptance Criteria
   124→
   125→**Given-When-Then Scenarios**:
   126→```gherkin
   127→Scenario 1: Helm lint passes on valid chart
   128→  Given a consumer workflow calls helm-lint.yml with a valid chart_path
   129→  When the workflow runs helm lint with the provided values files
   130→  Then the lint_status output is "success"
   131→  And the step summary shows a passing lint result
   132→
   133→Scenario 2: Helm lint fails on invalid chart
   134→  Given a consumer workflow calls helm-lint.yml with a chart containing errors
   135→  When the workflow runs helm lint
   136→  Then the lint_status output is "failure"
   137→  And the step summary shows the lint failure
   138→
   139→Scenario 3: Helm template validates rendered output
   140→  Given a consumer workflow calls helm-lint.yml with a valid chart
   141→  When the workflow runs helm template
   142→  Then the template_status output is "success"
   143→  And the rendered YAML is valid
   144→
   145→Scenario 4: Strict mode is configurable
   146→  Given a consumer workflow calls helm-lint.yml with strict: true
   147→  When the workflow runs helm lint
   148→  Then the --strict flag is passed to helm lint
   149→
   150→Scenario 5: Multiple values files are supported
   151→  Given a consumer workflow passes "values.yaml,values-dev.yaml" as values_files
   152→  When the workflow constructs the helm lint command
   153→  Then it uses "-f values.yaml -f values-dev.yaml" flags
   154→```
   155→
   156→**Rule-Based Criteria (Checklist)**:
   157→- [ ] Workflow file is valid YAML (passes `python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open(f))"`)
   158→- [ ] Workflow `name:` starts with `z_` (e.g., `z_Helm Lint (Reusable)`)
   159→- [ ] Trigger is `on: workflow_call:` with defined inputs and outputs
   160→- [ ] Job runs on `static-ip-runner`
   161→- [ ] No GCP authentication steps are included
   162→- [ ] `values_files` input is correctly parsed from comma-separated string to multiple `-f` flags
   163→- [ ] Both `helm lint` and `helm template` steps are present
   164→- [ ] Outputs `lint_status` and `template_status` are set correctly
   165→- [ ] `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` is generated with a markdown results table
   166→- [ ] `azure/setup-helm@v4` is used to install Helm
   167→
   168→#### Validation & Quality Gates
   169→
   170→```bash
   171→# Validate YAML syntax
   172→python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/helm-lint.yml'))"
   173→
   174→# Validate naming convention
   175→NAME=$(python3 -c "import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/helm-lint.yml'))['name'])")
   176→echo "$NAME" | grep -q "^z_" && echo "PASS: Name starts with z_" || echo "FAIL: Name does not start with z_"
   177→
   178→# Validate trigger type
   179→python3 -c "
   180→import yaml
   181→wf = yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/helm-lint.yml'))
   182→assert 'workflow_call' in wf[True], 'Missing workflow_call trigger'
   183→print('PASS: workflow_call trigger present')
   184→"
   185→```
   186→
   187→#### Resources & References
   188→
   189→- **Validation pattern**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml` -- summary format, validation job structure
   190→- **Helm setup**: `azure/setup-helm@v4` -- official Helm setup action
   191→- **Consumer example**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/.github/workflows/validate-plan-apply.yml` -- how caller repos invoke shared workflows

   194→
   195→### T-002: Create Helm Values Check Reusable Workflow
   196→
   197→**Task ID**: T-002
   198→**Task Name**: Create `helm-values-check.yml` reusable workflow for generated Helm values validation
   199→**Priority**: High
   200→
   201→#### Context & Background
   202→
   203→**Source PRP Document**: [docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md](/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md) -- Task 3
   204→
   205→**Feature Overview**: This workflow validates that committed `values-{env}.yaml` files match what `generate-helm-values.sh` would produce from `config.json`. It detects configuration drift where someone edits values files directly instead of updating `config.json` and regenerating.
   206→
   207→**Task Purpose**:
   208→- **As a** workload repository CI/CD pipeline
   209→- **I need** a reusable workflow that validates generated Helm values match committed values
   210→- **So that** configuration drift between `config.json` (source of truth) and `values-{env}.yaml` (generated) is detected early
   211→
   212→#### Dependencies
   213→- **Prerequisite Tasks**: None (simple workflow, no GCP auth needed)
   214→- **Parallel Tasks**: T-001 (helm-lint.yml)
   215→- **Integration Points**: Consumer repos must have `generate-helm-values.sh` script with `--check` flag support
   216→- **Blocked By**: None
   217→
   218→#### Technical Requirements
   219→
   220→**Functional Requirements**:
   221→- **REQ-1**: When called via `workflow_call`, the workflow shall accept `environment` and `script_path` as inputs
   222→- **REQ-2**: The workflow shall run the specified script with the `--check` flag for the given environment
   223→- **REQ-3**: When the script exits 0, the workflow shall set `check_status` output to "success"
   224→- **REQ-4**: When the script exits non-zero (drift detected), the workflow shall set `check_status` output to "failure"
   225→- **REQ-5**: The workflow shall generate a `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with check result and drift details if any
   226→
   227→**Non-Functional Requirements**:
   228→- **Runner**: Must use `static-ip-runner`
   229→- **Naming**: Must follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention
   230→- **Permissions**: Only `contents: read` required
   231→- **Dependencies**: `jq` must be available (required by `generate-helm-values.sh`)
   232→
   233→#### Implementation Details
   234→
   235→**Files to Create**:
   236→```
   237→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-values-check.yml - Helm values drift detection workflow
   238→```
   239→
   240→**Key Implementation Steps**:
   241→1. **Define workflow_call interface** -- inputs: `environment` (string, required), `script_path` (string, default: "./scripts/generate-helm-values.sh"); outputs: `check_status`
   242→2. **Set permissions** -- `contents: read` only
   243→3. **Create `check` job** on `static-ip-runner`:
   244→   - Checkout with `actions/checkout@v4`
   245→   - Verify `jq` is installed (should be on static-ip-runner; add install step as fallback)
   246→   - Verify script exists at `script_path` and is executable
   247→   - Run `bash $script_path $environment --check`
   248→   - Capture exit code and set `check_status` output
   249→4. **Generate step summary** -- Show check result, environment, and any diff output if drift detected
   250→5. **Handle failure gracefully** -- Capture script stderr/stdout for drift details in summary
   251→
   252→**Code Patterns to Follow**:
   253→- **Validation pattern**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml` -- validation + summary format
   254→- **Script reference**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/scripts/generate-helm-values.sh` -- the script this workflow invokes
   255→- **Config reference**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/environments/dev/config.json` -- `config.json` is the SSOT that the script reads
   256→
   257→#### Acceptance Criteria
   258→
   259→**Given-When-Then Scenarios**:
   260→```gherkin
   261→Scenario 1: Values match (no drift)
   262→  Given a consumer repo has config.json and matching values-dev.yaml
   263→  When the workflow runs generate-helm-values.sh dev --check
   264→  Then the script exits 0
   265→  And check_status output is "success"
   266→  And the step summary shows "Values are in sync"
   267→
   268→Scenario 2: Values drift detected
   269→  Given a consumer repo has config.json and a manually-edited values-dev.yaml
   270→  When the workflow runs generate-helm-values.sh dev --check
   271→  Then the script exits non-zero
   272→  And check_status output is "failure"
   273→  And the step summary shows the diff between expected and committed values
   274→
   275→Scenario 3: Script not found
   276→  Given a consumer repo does not have generate-helm-values.sh at the specified path
   277→  When the workflow attempts to run the script
   278→  Then the workflow fails with a clear error message
   279→  And the step summary indicates the script was not found
   280→
   281→Scenario 4: Custom script path
   282→  Given a consumer workflow passes script_path: "./tools/gen-values.sh"
   283→  When the workflow runs
   284→  Then it uses the custom path instead of the default
   285→```
   286→
   287→**Rule-Based Criteria (Checklist)**:
   288→- [ ] Workflow file is valid YAML
   289→- [ ] Workflow `name:` starts with `z_` (e.g., `z_Helm Values Check (Reusable)`)
   290→- [ ] Trigger is `on: workflow_call:` with defined inputs and outputs
   291→- [ ] Job runs on `static-ip-runner`
   292→- [ ] No GCP authentication steps are included
   293→- [ ] `jq` availability is verified or installed
   294→- [ ] Script existence is verified before execution
   295→- [ ] `check_status` output is set correctly based on script exit code
   296→- [ ] `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` is generated with check result
   297→- [ ] Drift details (diff output) are captured and displayed in summary on failure
   298→
   299→#### Validation & Quality Gates
   300→
   301→```bash
   302→# Validate YAML syntax
   303→python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/helm-values-check.yml'))"
   304→
   305→# Validate naming convention
   306→NAME=$(python3 -c "import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/helm-values-check.yml'))['name'])")
   307→echo "$NAME" | grep -q "^z_" && echo "PASS" || echo "FAIL"
   308→
   309→# Validate no GCP auth steps
   310→python3 -c "
   311→import yaml
   312→wf = yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/helm-values-check.yml'))
   313→job = list(wf['jobs'].values())[0]
   314→step_names = [s.get('name', '') for s in job['steps']]
   315→assert not any('gcp' in n.lower() or 'oidc' in n.lower() for n in step_names), 'Should not have GCP auth'
   316→print('PASS: No GCP auth steps')
   317→"
   318→```
   319→
   320→#### Resources & References
   321→
   322→- **Validation pattern**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml`
   323→- **Script reference**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/scripts/generate-helm-values.sh`
   324→- **Config schema**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/environments/dev/config.json`

   327→
   328→### T-003: Create Docker Build, Scan, Sign, and Push Reusable Workflow
   329→
   330→**Task ID**: T-003
   331→**Task Name**: Create `docker-build-push.yml` reusable workflow for Docker image build pipeline
   332→**Priority**: Critical
   333→
   334→#### Context & Background
   335→
   336→**Source PRP Document**: [docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md](/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md) -- Task 1
   337→
   338→**Feature Overview**: This is the most complex of the four new workflows. It provides a complete Docker image build pipeline: multi-stage build with caching, Trivy vulnerability scanning, Cosign keyless signing, and push to Artifact Registry. It handles GCP OIDC authentication self-contained, so consumer repos only need to set `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` and `vars.SERVICE_NAME` as GitHub Environment variables.
   339→
   340→**Task Purpose**:
   341→- **As a** workload or application repository CI/CD pipeline
   342→- **I need** a reusable workflow that builds, scans, signs, and pushes Docker images to Artifact Registry
   343→- **So that** all ~24 services get production-grade container security without duplicating complex build logic
   344→
   345→#### Dependencies
   346→- **Prerequisite Tasks**: T-001, T-002 (simpler workflows first to establish patterns; not a hard technical dependency)
   347→- **Parallel Tasks**: None (this is the critical path for the Docker build capability)
   348→- **Integration Points**: GCP Workload Identity Federation, Artifact Registry (`europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images`), GitHub Code Scanning (SARIF upload), Cosign/Sigstore OIDC
   349→- **Blocked By**: None
   350→
   351→#### Technical Requirements
   352→
   353→**Functional Requirements**:
   354→- **REQ-1**: When called via `workflow_call`, the workflow shall accept `image_name`, `dockerfile_path`, `build_context`, `environment`, `push`, `enable_trivy`, `trivy_severity`, `enable_cosign`, and `build_args` as inputs
   355→- **REQ-2**: The workflow shall authenticate to GCP using OIDC/WIF self-contained (using `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` and `vars.SERVICE_NAME`)
   356→- **REQ-3**: The workflow shall configure Docker authentication for `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev` using `gcloud auth configure-docker`
   357→- **REQ-4**: The workflow shall build images using `docker/build-push-action@v6` with `docker/setup-buildx-action@v3`
   358→- **REQ-5**: The workflow shall use GitHub Actions cache (`cache-from: type=gha`, `cache-to: type=gha`) for layer caching
   359→- **REQ-6**: When `enable_trivy` is true, the workflow shall scan the built image with `aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1` and upload SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning
   360→- **REQ-7**: When `enable_cosign` is true and scan passes, the workflow shall sign the image using `sigstore/cosign-installer@v3` with keyless signing (`cosign sign --yes`)
   361→- **REQ-8**: The workflow shall output `image_uri`, `image_tag`, `trivy_findings_count`, `trivy_critical_count`, `trivy_high_count`, and `scan_passed`
   362→- **REQ-9**: The workflow shall tag images with both `${{ github.sha }}` and `latest`
   363→- **REQ-10**: The workflow shall enable SBOM and provenance attestation on the build
   364→
   365→**Non-Functional Requirements**:
   366→- **Runner**: Must use `static-ip-runner`
   367→- **Security**: `id-token: write` for OIDC (GCP + Cosign), `security-events: write` for SARIF upload
   368→- **Naming**: Must follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention
   369→
   370→**Technical Constraints**:
   371→- GCP auth uses `vars` (environment variables), NOT `secrets`
   372→- WIF pool naming: `github-{SERVICE_NAME}-{ENV}`
   373→- Provider: `projects/{PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/{POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider`
   374→- Service account: `{POOL_ID}@{GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com`
   375→- DO NOT use `docker/login-action` for AR auth -- use `gcloud auth configure-docker` after OIDC auth
   376→- Trivy version pinned to `0.33.1` for consistency
   377→
   378→#### Implementation Details
   379→
   380→**Files to Create**:
   381→```
   382→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml - Docker build pipeline reusable workflow
   383→```
   384→
   385→**Key Implementation Steps**:
   386→
   387→1. **Define workflow_call interface**:
   388→   - inputs: `image_name` (string, required), `dockerfile_path` (string, default: "./Dockerfile"), `build_context` (string, default: "."), `environment` (string, required), `push` (boolean, default: true), `enable_trivy` (boolean, default: true), `trivy_severity` (string, default: "CRITICAL,HIGH"), `enable_cosign` (boolean, default: true), `build_args` (string, default: "")
   389→   - outputs: `image_uri`, `image_tag`, `trivy_findings_count`, `trivy_critical_count`, `trivy_high_count`, `scan_passed`
   390→
   391→2. **Set permissions**:
   392→   ```yaml
   393→   permissions:
   394→     id-token: write       # OIDC for GCP + Cosign
   395→     contents: read
   396→     security-events: write # SARIF upload
   397→     packages: write        # Container registry
   398→   ```
   399→
   400→3. **Create `build-push` job** on `static-ip-runner` with `environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}`:
   401→
   402→   **Step: Configure GCP Auth** (MIRROR `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116 exactly):
   403→   - Derive `POOL_ID` as `github-${{ vars.SERVICE_NAME }}-${{ inputs.environment }}`
   404→   - Derive `PROVIDER` as `projects/${{ vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER }}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider`
   405→   - Derive `SERVICE_ACCOUNT` as `${POOL_ID}@${{ vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID || format('kd-ix-eur-{0}-gke', inputs.environment) }}.iam.gserviceaccount.com`
   406→
   407→   **Step: Authenticate to GCP** -- `google-github-actions/auth@v2` with workload_identity_provider and service_account
   408→
   409→   **Step: Configure Docker for AR** -- `gcloud auth configure-docker europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev --quiet`
   410→
   411→   **Step: Setup Docker Buildx** -- `docker/setup-buildx-action@v3`
   412→
   413→   **Step: Generate image metadata**:
   414→   - `IMAGE_REPO=europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}`
   415→   - `TAG=${{ github.sha }}`
   416→   - `FULL_IMAGE=${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG}`
   417→
   418→   **Step: Build and Push** -- `docker/build-push-action@v6`:
   419→   - `context: ${{ inputs.build_context }}`
   420→   - `file: ${{ inputs.dockerfile_path }}`
   421→   - `push: ${{ inputs.push }}`
   422→   - `tags: ${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG},${IMAGE_REPO}:latest`
   423→   - `cache-from: type=gha`
   424→   - `cache-to: type=gha,mode=max`
   425→   - `provenance: true`
   426→   - `sbom: true`
   427→   - `build-args: ${{ inputs.build_args }}`
   428→
   429→   **Step: Trivy scan** (if `enable_trivy`):
   430→   - `aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1`
   431→   - `scan-type: image`
   432→   - `image-ref: ${FULL_IMAGE}`
   433→   - `severity: ${{ inputs.trivy_severity }}`
   434→   - Generate SARIF output + table output (dual format like `terraform-validate.yml`)
   435→   - Upload SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning with `github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v3`
   436→   - Parse findings count, critical count, high count
   437→   - Determine `scan_passed` based on findings
   438→
   439→   **Step: Cosign sign** (if `enable_cosign` and scan passed):
   440→   - `sigstore/cosign-installer@v3`
   441→   - `cosign sign --yes ${IMAGE_REPO}@${DIGEST}`
   442→   - CRITICAL: `--yes` flag for keyless/non-interactive signing
   443→
   444→   **Step: Generate summary** -- Markdown table with image URI, tag, digest, scan results, signing status
   445→
   446→**Code Patterns to Follow**:
   447→- **GCP OIDC auth**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml:82-116` -- MUST mirror this exact block
   448→- **Trivy scanning**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml` -- SARIF + table dual format
   449→- **Docker build**: `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/container-security.yaml` -- buildx, GHA cache, Trivy image scan, Cosign keyless
   450→- **Cosign signing**: `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-images/.github/workflows/sync-images.yml` -- Cosign signing with WIF
   451→
   452→#### Acceptance Criteria
   453→
   454→**Given-When-Then Scenarios**:
   455→```gherkin
   456→Scenario 1: Successful build, scan, sign, and push
   457→  Given a consumer workflow calls docker-build-push.yml with image_name and environment
   458→  When the workflow runs with a valid Dockerfile
   459→  Then the image is built and pushed to europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/{image_name}
   460→  And the image is scanned by Trivy with SARIF uploaded to GitHub Code Scanning
   461→  And the image is signed with Cosign keyless signing
   462→  And image_uri and image_tag outputs are set
   463→
   464→Scenario 2: Trivy scan finds critical vulnerabilities
   465→  Given a consumer workflow calls docker-build-push.yml with enable_trivy: true
   466→  When the built image contains CRITICAL severity vulnerabilities
   467→  Then scan_passed output is "false"
   468→  And trivy_critical_count output reflects the count
   469→  And the step summary shows vulnerability details
   470→  And Cosign signing is skipped (scan did not pass)
   471→
   472→Scenario 3: GCP OIDC auth uses environment variables
   473→  Given a consumer repo has vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER and vars.SERVICE_NAME configured
   474→  When the workflow runs for environment "dev"
   475→  Then WIF pool ID is derived as "github-{SERVICE_NAME}-dev"
   476→  And authentication succeeds without any secrets being passed
   477→
   478→Scenario 4: Build caching with GHA cache
   479→  Given a consumer workflow calls docker-build-push.yml
   480→  When a previous build exists in GHA cache
   481→  Then Docker layers are reused from cache
   482→  And build time is reduced
   483→
   484→Scenario 5: Trivy and Cosign are individually toggleable
   485→  Given a consumer workflow calls docker-build-push.yml with enable_trivy: false
   486→  When the workflow runs
   487→  Then the Trivy scan step is skipped
   488→  And the Cosign sign step still runs (independent toggle)
   489→
   490→Scenario 6: Custom build args are passed through
   491→  Given a consumer workflow passes build_args with "NODE_ENV=production\nAPP_VERSION=1.2.3"
   492→  When the workflow runs docker/build-push-action
   493→  Then the build args are passed to the Docker build
   494→```
   495→
   496→**Rule-Based Criteria (Checklist)**:
   497→- [ ] Workflow file is valid YAML
   498→- [ ] Workflow `name:` starts with `z_` (e.g., `z_Docker Build Push (Reusable)`)
   499→- [ ] Trigger is `on: workflow_call:` with all defined inputs and outputs
   500→- [ ] Job runs on `static-ip-runner`
   501→- [ ] GCP OIDC auth block mirrors `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116 exactly
   502→- [ ] Uses `gcloud auth configure-docker` (NOT `docker/login-action`)
   503→- [ ] Uses `docker/setup-buildx-action@v3`
   504→- [ ] Uses `docker/build-push-action@v6` with `cache-from: type=gha` and `cache-to: type=gha,mode=max`
   505→- [ ] `provenance: true` and `sbom: true` are set on build
   506→- [ ] Trivy uses version `0.33.1` and outputs both SARIF and table format
   507→- [ ] SARIF is uploaded to GitHub Code Scanning via `github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v3`
   508→- [ ] Cosign uses `sigstore/cosign-installer@v3` and signs with `--yes` flag
   509→- [ ] Cosign signing requires `id-token: write` permission
   510→- [ ] All 6 outputs are set correctly
   511→- [ ] `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` is generated with image details and scan results
   512→- [ ] No hardcoded project IDs or registry paths -- all derived from inputs/vars
   513→- [ ] `vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER` and `vars.SERVICE_NAME` used for auth (not secrets)
   514→- [ ] Image tagged with both `${{ github.sha }}` and `latest`
   515→
   516→#### Validation & Quality Gates
   517→
   518→```bash
   519→# Validate YAML syntax
   520→python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml'))"
   521→
   522→# Validate naming convention
   523→NAME=$(python3 -c "import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml'))['name'])")
   524→echo "$NAME" | grep -q "^z_" && echo "PASS" || echo "FAIL"
   525→
   526→# Validate GCP auth uses vars not secrets
   527→grep -c "vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER" .github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml && echo "PASS: Uses vars for auth" || echo "FAIL"
   528→grep -c "secrets.GCP" .github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml && echo "FAIL: Using secrets for GCP" || echo "PASS: No GCP secrets"
   529→
   530→# Validate static-ip-runner
   531→grep -q "static-ip-runner" .github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml && echo "PASS" || echo "FAIL: Not using static-ip-runner"
   532→
   533→# Validate cosign --yes flag
   534→grep -q "\-\-yes" .github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml && echo "PASS: cosign --yes present" || echo "FAIL: Missing --yes flag"
   535→```
   536→
   537→#### Resources & References
   538→
   539→- **GCP OIDC auth pattern**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml:82-116`
   540→- **Trivy scanning**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-validate.yml`
   541→- **Docker build reference**: `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/container-security.yaml`
   542→- **Cosign reference**: `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-images/.github/workflows/sync-images.yml`
   543→- **Consumer pattern**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/.github/workflows/validate-plan-apply.yml`
   544→
   545→#### Implementation Notes
   546→
   547→- The GCP auth block is the most critical part. It must match `terraform-plan.yml` exactly so that the same WIF setup works for both Terraform and Docker workflows.
   548→- The Artifact Registry path `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images` is shared across all environments. Images are differentiated by tags, not registries.
   549→- Cosign keyless signing uses the GitHub Actions OIDC token as the identity. The `id-token: write` permission is essential.
   550→- The `--yes` flag on `cosign sign` is required for non-interactive execution in CI. Without it, Cosign prompts for confirmation and the workflow hangs.
   551→
   552→---
   553→
   554→### T-004: Create Image Promotion Reusable Workflow
   555→
   556→**Task ID**: T-004
   557→**Task Name**: Create `image-promote.yml` reusable workflow for image promotion across environments
   558→**Priority**: Critical
   559→
   560→#### Context & Background
   561→
   562→**Source PRP Document**: [docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md](/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md) -- Task 4
   563→
   564→**Feature Overview**: This workflow handles promoting container images from one environment to another (dev to qa to prod). It performs two operations: (1) re-tags the image in Artifact Registry, and (2) creates a PR in the workload repository to update `config.json` with the new image tag. This is the most complex workflow because it involves both GCP Artifact Registry operations and cross-repository Git operations.
   565→
   566→**Task Purpose**:
   567→- **As a** release engineer or CI/CD pipeline
   568→- **I need** a reusable workflow that promotes images across environments by re-tagging in AR and updating workload repo config
   569→- **So that** the promotion process is standardized, auditable (via PR), and includes approval gates for production
   570→
   571→#### Dependencies
   572→- **Prerequisite Tasks**: T-003 (docker-build-push.yml -- establishes GCP auth pattern for AR access)
   573→- **Parallel Tasks**: None
   574→- **Integration Points**: GCP Artifact Registry (image re-tagging), GitHub App tokens (cross-repo access), workload repo `config.json` and `generate-helm-values.sh`, GitHub Environment protection rules (approval gates)
   575→- **Blocked By**: None (but requires GitHub App to be configured for cross-repo access)
   576→
   577→#### Technical Requirements
   578→
   579→**Functional Requirements**:
   580→- **REQ-1**: When called via `workflow_call`, the workflow shall accept `image_name`, `source_tag`, `target_environment`, `workload_repo`, `config_path`, and `require_approval` as inputs
   581→- **REQ-2**: The workflow shall accept `WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID` and `WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY` as secrets for cross-repo access
   582→- **REQ-3**: The workflow shall verify the source image exists in Artifact Registry before proceeding
   583→- **REQ-4**: The workflow shall re-tag the image in AR using `gcloud artifacts docker tags add`
   584→- **REQ-5**: The workflow shall generate a GitHub App token for cross-repo operations
   585→- **REQ-6**: The workflow shall clone the workload repo, update `config.json` image tag using `jq`, and create a PR
   586→- **REQ-7**: When `require_approval` is true, the workflow shall use GitHub Environment protection rules for approval gates
   587→- **REQ-8**: The workflow shall output `promoted_image_uri`, `pr_url`, and `pr_number`
   588→- **REQ-9**: The PR branch shall follow the naming convention `promote/{image_name}/{target_environment}/{source_tag}`
   589→- **REQ-10**: The PR title shall follow the convention `promote: {image_name} to {target_environment} ({source_tag})`
   590→- **REQ-11**: The workflow shall update `config.json` (the SSOT), NOT `values-{env}.yaml` directly
   591→
   592→**Non-Functional Requirements**:
   593→- **Runner**: Must use `static-ip-runner`
   594→- **Security**: `id-token: write` for GCP OIDC, cross-repo access via GitHub App token (not PAT)
   595→- **Naming**: Must follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention
   596→- **Approval**: Production promotions should require manual approval via GitHub Environment protection
   597→
   598→**Technical Constraints**:
   599→- `gcloud artifacts docker tags add` requires `roles/artifactregistry.writer` on the WIF service account
   600→- Cross-repo PR creation needs `contents: write` and `pull-requests: write` on the target repo
   601→- Must use a GitHub App token (via `actions/create-github-app-token@v1`) for cross-repo access
   602→- `config.json` field to update: `.service.image.tag`
   603→- After updating `config.json`, optionally run `generate-helm-values.sh` if it exists in the workload repo
   604→
   605→#### Implementation Details
   606→
   607→**Files to Create**:
   608→```
   609→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/image-promote.yml - Image promotion reusable workflow
   610→```
   611→
   612→**Key Implementation Steps**:
   613→
   614→1. **Define workflow_call interface**:
   615→   - inputs: `image_name` (string, required), `source_tag` (string, required), `target_environment` (string, required), `workload_repo` (string, required), `config_path` (string, required), `require_approval` (boolean, default: true)
   616→   - secrets: `WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID` (required), `WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY` (required)
   617→   - outputs: `promoted_image_uri`, `pr_url`, `pr_number`
   618→
   619→2. **Set permissions**:
   620→   ```yaml
   621→   permissions:
   622→     id-token: write   # GCP OIDC for AR
   623→     contents: read
   624→   ```
   625→
   626→3. **Create `promote` job** on `static-ip-runner`:
   627→   - Set `environment: ${{ inputs.require_approval && inputs.target_environment || '' }}` for approval gates
   628→
   629→   **Step: Configure GCP Auth** (MIRROR `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116):
   630→   - Same OIDC/WIF pattern as T-003
   631→
   632→   **Step: Authenticate to GCP** -- `google-github-actions/auth@v2`
   633→
   634→   **Step: Verify source image exists**:
   635→   ```bash
   636→   gcloud artifacts docker images describe \
   637→     europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}:${{ inputs.source_tag }}
   638→   ```
   639→
   640→   **Step: Add target environment tag**:
   641→   ```bash
   642→   gcloud artifacts docker tags add \
   643→     europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}:${{ inputs.source_tag }} \
   644→     europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}:${{ inputs.target_environment }}-${{ inputs.source_tag }}
   645→   ```
   646→
   647→   **Step: Generate GitHub App token** -- `actions/create-github-app-token@v1` with `WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID` and `WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY`, scoped to the workload repo
   648→
   649→   **Step: Clone workload repo**:
   650→   ```bash
   651→   git clone https://x-access-token:${TOKEN}@github.com/${{ inputs.workload_repo }}.git workload-repo
   652→   ```
   653→
   654→   **Step: Update config.json image tag**:
   655→   ```bash
   656→   cd workload-repo
   657→   jq '.service.image.tag = "${{ inputs.source_tag }}"' ${{ inputs.config_path }} > tmp.json && mv tmp.json ${{ inputs.config_path }}
   658→   ```
   659→
   660→   **Step: Regenerate Helm values** (if script exists):
   661→   ```bash
   662→   if [ -f scripts/generate-helm-values.sh ]; then
   663→     bash scripts/generate-helm-values.sh ${{ inputs.target_environment }}
   664→   fi
   665→   ```
   666→
   667→   **Step: Create PR** via `gh pr create` or `peter-evans/create-pull-request@v6`:
   668→   - Branch: `promote/${{ inputs.image_name }}/${{ inputs.target_environment }}/${{ inputs.source_tag }}`
   669→   - Title: `promote: ${{ inputs.image_name }} to ${{ inputs.target_environment }} (${{ inputs.source_tag }})`
   670→   - Body: Include source tag, target environment, image URI, promotion timestamp
   671→
   672→   **Step: Generate summary** -- Markdown with promoted image URI, target environment, PR link, approval status
   673→
   674→**Code Patterns to Follow**:
   675→- **GCP OIDC auth**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml:82-116`
   676→- **Environment approval**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-apply.yml` -- environment input for approval gates
   677→- **PR creation**: `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/promote-release.yaml` -- PR creation via `peter-evans/create-pull-request@v6`
   678→- **Image tagging**: `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-images/.github/workflows/sync-images.yml` -- AR image tagging
   679→
   680→#### Acceptance Criteria
   681→
   682→**Given-When-Then Scenarios**:
   683→```gherkin
   684→Scenario 1: Successful promotion from dev to qa
   685→  Given a consumer workflow calls image-promote.yml with source_tag from a dev build
   686→  When the workflow runs for target_environment "qa"
   687→  Then the image is re-tagged in AR with "qa-{source_tag}"
   688→  And a PR is created in the workload repo updating config.json with the new image tag
   689→  And promoted_image_uri, pr_url, and pr_number outputs are set
   690→
   691→Scenario 2: Source image does not exist
   692→  Given a consumer workflow calls image-promote.yml with an invalid source_tag
   693→  When the workflow attempts to verify the source image
   694→  Then the workflow fails with a clear error indicating the image was not found
   695→  And no PR is created
   696→
   697→Scenario 3: Production promotion requires approval
   698→  Given a consumer workflow calls image-promote.yml with target_environment "prod" and require_approval: true
   699→  When the workflow starts
   700→  Then it pauses at the GitHub Environment protection gate
   701→  And only proceeds after manual approval
   702→
   703→Scenario 4: config.json is updated (not values-{env}.yaml)
   704→  Given a consumer workflow calls image-promote.yml
   705→  When the workflow updates the workload repo
   706→  Then config.json .service.image.tag is updated with the source_tag
   707→  And values-{env}.yaml is regenerated from config.json (if generate-helm-values.sh exists)
   708→  And the PR diff shows both config.json and values-{env}.yaml changes
   709→
   710→Scenario 5: PR follows naming conventions
   711→  Given a consumer workflow calls image-promote.yml with image_name "kix-app-iqportal" and target_environment "qa"
   712→  When the PR is created
   713→  Then the branch name is "promote/kix-app-iqportal/qa/{source_tag}"
   714→  And the PR title is "promote: kix-app-iqportal to qa ({source_tag})"
   715→
   716→Scenario 6: Cross-repo access uses GitHub App token
   717→  Given a consumer workflow passes WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID and WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY secrets
   718→  When the workflow generates an access token
   719→  Then it uses actions/create-github-app-token@v1
   720→  And the token is scoped to the workload repo only
   721→```
   722→
   723→**Rule-Based Criteria (Checklist)**:
   724→- [ ] Workflow file is valid YAML
   725→- [ ] Workflow `name:` starts with `z_` (e.g., `z_Image Promote (Reusable)`)
   726→- [ ] Trigger is `on: workflow_call:` with all defined inputs, secrets, and outputs
   727→- [ ] Job runs on `static-ip-runner`
   728→- [ ] GCP OIDC auth block mirrors `terraform-plan.yml` exactly
   729→- [ ] Source image existence is verified before re-tagging
   730→- [ ] `gcloud artifacts docker tags add` is used for re-tagging (not push)
   731→- [ ] GitHub App token is generated via `actions/create-github-app-token@v1`
   732→- [ ] `config.json` is updated using `jq` (NOT direct edit of `values-{env}.yaml`)
   733→- [ ] `generate-helm-values.sh` is run if it exists in the workload repo
   734→- [ ] PR is created (not a direct commit) for config changes
   735→- [ ] PR branch follows `promote/{image_name}/{target_environment}/{source_tag}` convention
   736→- [ ] `environment` field enables GitHub Environment protection for approval gates
   737→- [ ] All 3 outputs are set correctly
   738→- [ ] `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` is generated with promotion details and PR link
   739→- [ ] No hardcoded project IDs, registry paths, or repo names
   740→- [ ] Cross-repo access uses GitHub App token, not PAT
   741→
   742→#### Validation & Quality Gates
   743→
   744→```bash
   745→# Validate YAML syntax
   746→python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/image-promote.yml'))"
   747→
   748→# Validate naming convention
   749→NAME=$(python3 -c "import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/image-promote.yml'))['name'])")
   750→echo "$NAME" | grep -q "^z_" && echo "PASS" || echo "FAIL"
   751→
   752→# Validate secrets are defined (not vars for App credentials)
   753→python3 -c "
   754→import yaml
   755→wf = yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/image-promote.yml'))
   756→secrets = wf[True]['workflow_call'].get('secrets', {})
   757→assert 'WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID' in secrets, 'Missing WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID secret'
   758→assert 'WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY' in secrets, 'Missing WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY secret'
   759→print('PASS: Required secrets defined')
   760→"
   761→
   762→# Validate config.json update (not values-{env}.yaml direct edit)
   763→grep -q "config.json" .github/workflows/image-promote.yml && echo "PASS: Updates config.json" || echo "FAIL"
   764→```
   765→
   766→#### Resources & References
   767→
   768→- **GCP OIDC auth**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml:82-116`
   769→- **Approval gates**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-apply.yml`
   770→- **PR creation**: `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai/.github/workflows.old/promote-release.yaml`
   771→- **Image tagging**: `konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-images/.github/workflows/sync-images.yml`
   772→- **Config schema**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/environments/dev/config.json`
   773→- **Values generation**: `/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/scripts/generate-helm-values.sh`
   774→
   775→#### Implementation Notes
   776→
   777→- The `environment` field on the job enables GitHub Environment protection rules. When `require_approval` is true, the job will pause and wait for manual approval before proceeding. This is particularly important for production promotions.
   778→- The image re-tagging in AR does not create a new image layer -- it just adds a new tag pointing to the same digest. This is fast and does not consume additional storage.
   779→- The PR creation approach ensures auditability. Direct commits to workload repos bypass review and are an anti-pattern.
   780→- The GitHub App token must have `contents: write` and `pull-requests: write` permissions on the target workload repo.
   781→- When `generate-helm-values.sh` exists in the workload repo, it should be run after updating `config.json` so that the PR includes both the config change and the regenerated values file. This ensures the PR is self-contained and mergeable without additional steps.
   782→
   783→---
   784→
   785→### T-005: Validate All Workflows Against Conventions and Integration Requirements
   786→
   787→**Task ID**: T-005
   788→**Task Name**: End-to-end validation of all 4 reusable workflows against PRP requirements
   789→**Priority**: High
   790→
   791→#### Context & Background
   792→
   793→**Source PRP Document**: [docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md](/home/nathan_booth/code/kix-workload-konectaiq/docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md) -- Validation Loop + Final Validation Checklist
   794→
   795→**Feature Overview**: This task ensures all four workflows are consistent, follow conventions, and meet the PRP's final validation checklist. It also verifies cross-workflow consistency (e.g., GCP auth blocks are identical, naming conventions are uniform, output patterns are consistent).
   796→
   797→**Task Purpose**:
   798→- **As a** platform engineering team
   799→- **I need** comprehensive validation that all 4 workflows meet quality, security, and convention standards
   800→- **So that** the workflows can be merged with confidence and consumed by workload repos without issues
   801→
   802→#### Dependencies
   803→- **Prerequisite Tasks**: T-001, T-002, T-003, T-004 (all 4 workflows must be created first)
   804→- **Parallel Tasks**: None
   805→- **Integration Points**: All 4 workflow files, existing workflows in `.github-private`, PRP validation checklist
   806→- **Blocked By**: T-001 through T-004
   807→
   808→#### Technical Requirements
   809→
   810→**Functional Requirements**:
   811→- **REQ-1**: All 4 YAML files shall pass syntax validation
   812→- **REQ-2**: All workflow names shall follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention
   813→- **REQ-3**: All workflows shall use `on: workflow_call:` trigger
   814→- **REQ-4**: All workflows shall run on `static-ip-runner`
   815→- **REQ-5**: `docker-build-push.yml` and `image-promote.yml` shall have identical GCP OIDC auth blocks matching `terraform-plan.yml`
   816→- **REQ-6**: `helm-lint.yml` and `helm-values-check.yml` shall NOT contain GCP auth steps
   817→- **REQ-7**: All workflows shall define structured `outputs:` for downstream consumption
   818→- **REQ-8**: All workflows shall generate `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with markdown tables
   819→- **REQ-9**: `docker-build-push.yml` shall upload Trivy SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning
   820→- **REQ-10**: `image-promote.yml` shall create a PR (not a direct commit) for config updates
   821→- **REQ-11**: No hardcoded project IDs, registry paths, or service names in any workflow
   822→
   823→#### Implementation Details
   824→
   825→**Files to Validate**:
   826→```
   827→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml
   828→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-lint.yml
   829→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-values-check.yml
   830→konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/image-promote.yml
   831→```
   832→
   833→**Key Validation Steps**:
   834→
   835→1. **YAML Syntax Validation**:
   836→   ```bash
   837→   for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   838→     python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/$f'))"
   839→   done
   840→   ```
   841→
   842→2. **Naming Convention Validation**:
   843→   ```bash
   844→   for f in .github/workflows/*.yml; do
   845→     NAME=$(python3 -c "import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('$f'))['name'])")
   846→     echo "$f -> $NAME"
   847→     echo "$NAME" | grep -q "^z_" || echo "FAIL: $f name does not start with z_"
   848→   done
   849→   ```
   850→
   851→3. **Runner Validation**:
   852→   ```bash
   853→   for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   854→     grep -q "static-ip-runner" .github/workflows/$f || echo "FAIL: $f not using static-ip-runner"
   855→   done
   856→   ```
   857→
   858→4. **GCP Auth Consistency** -- Verify that docker-build-push.yml and image-promote.yml have identical auth blocks, and that helm-lint.yml and helm-values-check.yml do not have any GCP auth
   859→
   860→5. **Output Definitions** -- Verify each workflow defines the expected outputs per the PRP interface specification
   861→
   862→6. **Step Summary Generation** -- Verify each workflow writes to `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`
   863→
   864→7. **Anti-Pattern Check**:
   865→   - No `ubuntu-latest` in any workflow
   866→   - No `docker/login-action` in docker-build-push.yml
   867→   - No hardcoded `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images`
   868→   - No `secrets.GCP_*` (should use `vars.GCP_*`)
   869→   - No direct commits in image-promote.yml (must use PR)
   870→
   871→**Code Patterns to Follow**:
   872→- PRP Final Validation Checklist -- all items must pass
   873→- PRP Anti-Patterns to Avoid -- none should be present
   874→
   875→#### Acceptance Criteria
   876→
   877→**Given-When-Then Scenarios**:
   878→```gherkin
   879→Scenario 1: All YAML files are syntactically valid
   880→  Given all 4 workflow files exist in .github/workflows/
   881→  When each file is parsed with python3 yaml.safe_load
   882→  Then no errors are raised for any file
   883→
   884→Scenario 2: All workflows follow naming convention
   885→  Given all 4 workflow files are loaded
   886→  When the 'name' field is extracted from each
   887→  Then all names match the pattern "z_<Name> (Reusable)"
   888→
   889→Scenario 3: GCP auth is correctly scoped
   890→  Given docker-build-push.yml and image-promote.yml contain GCP auth
   891→  When the auth blocks are compared to terraform-plan.yml
   892→  Then the auth pattern matches exactly
   893→  And helm-lint.yml and helm-values-check.yml contain no GCP auth steps
   894→
   895→Scenario 4: No anti-patterns are present
   896→  Given all 4 workflow files are inspected
   897→  When checked against the PRP anti-patterns list
   898→  Then no anti-patterns are found
   899→  And all workflows use static-ip-runner
   900→  And no hardcoded project IDs exist
   901→
   902→Scenario 5: Consumer workflow integration works
   903→  Given all 4 workflows are callable via workflow_call
   904→  When a consumer repo references them with the full cross-org path
   905→  Then the input/output interfaces are complete and consistent
   906→  And all required inputs have appropriate defaults where applicable
   907→```
   908→
   909→**Rule-Based Criteria (Checklist)** -- PRP Final Validation Checklist:
   910→- [ ] All 4 YAML files pass syntax validation
   911→- [ ] All workflow names follow `z_<Name> (Reusable)` convention
   912→- [ ] All workflows use `on: workflow_call:` trigger
   913→- [ ] All workflows run on `static-ip-runner`
   914→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` and `image-promote.yml` implement self-contained GCP OIDC auth matching `terraform-plan.yml` pattern
   915→- [ ] `helm-lint.yml` and `helm-values-check.yml` do NOT require GCP auth
   916→- [ ] All workflows define structured `outputs:` for downstream consumption
   917→- [ ] All workflows generate `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` with markdown tables
   918→- [ ] `docker-build-push.yml` uploads Trivy SARIF to GitHub Code Scanning
   919→- [ ] `image-promote.yml` creates a PR (not a direct commit) for config updates
   920→- [ ] No hardcoded project IDs, registry paths, or service names -- all parameterized via inputs or vars
   921→- [ ] No `ubuntu-latest` runner references
   922→- [ ] No `docker/login-action` usage (must use `gcloud auth configure-docker`)
   923→- [ ] `cosign sign` uses `--yes` flag
   924→- [ ] `vars` used for GCP credentials, not `secrets`
   925→
   926→#### Validation & Quality Gates
   927→
   928→```bash
   929→# Complete validation script
   930→echo "=== YAML Syntax Validation ==="
   931→for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   932→  python3 -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/$f'))" && echo "PASS: $f" || echo "FAIL: $f"
   933→done
   934→
   935→echo "=== Naming Convention ==="
   936→for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   937→  NAME=$(python3 -c "import yaml; print(yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/$f'))['name'])")
   938→  echo "$NAME" | grep -q "^z_" && echo "PASS: $f -> $NAME" || echo "FAIL: $f -> $NAME"
   939→done
   940→
   941→echo "=== Runner Check ==="
   942→for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   943→  grep -q "static-ip-runner" .github/workflows/$f && echo "PASS: $f" || echo "FAIL: $f"
   944→  grep -q "ubuntu-latest" .github/workflows/$f && echo "FAIL: $f uses ubuntu-latest" || echo "PASS: $f no ubuntu-latest"
   945→done
   946→
   947→echo "=== GCP Auth Scoping ==="
   948→for f in helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml; do
   949→  grep -qi "gcp\|oidc\|workload.identity" .github/workflows/$f && echo "FAIL: $f has GCP auth" || echo "PASS: $f no GCP auth"
   950→done
   951→for f in docker-build-push.yml image-promote.yml; do
   952→  grep -q "google-github-actions/auth" .github/workflows/$f && echo "PASS: $f has GCP auth" || echo "FAIL: $f missing GCP auth"
   953→done
   954→
   955→echo "=== Anti-Pattern Check ==="
   956→for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   957→  grep -q "docker/login-action" .github/workflows/$f && echo "FAIL: $f uses docker/login-action" || echo "PASS: $f"
   958→  grep -q "secrets.GCP" .github/workflows/$f && echo "FAIL: $f uses secrets for GCP" || echo "PASS: $f"
   959→done
   960→
   961→echo "=== Step Summary ==="
   962→for f in docker-build-push.yml helm-lint.yml helm-values-check.yml image-promote.yml; do
   963→  grep -q "GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY" .github/workflows/$f && echo "PASS: $f" || echo "FAIL: $f missing step summary"
   964→done
   965→```

   971→- **PRP Anti-Patterns**: `docs/prps/shared-github-actions-workflows.md` -- Anti-Patterns to Avoid section
   972→- **Existing workflows**: `konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-*.yml`, `argocd-deploy.yml`
   973→
   974→---
   975→
   976→## Implementation Recommendations
   977→
   978→### Suggested Task Sequencing
   979→
   980→```
   981→T-001 (helm-lint.yml) ──────────┐
   982→                                ├──> T-003 (docker-build-push.yml) ──> T-004 (image-promote.yml) ──> T-005 (validation)
   983→T-002 (helm-values-check.yml) ──┘
   984→```
   985→
   986→1. **Start with T-001 and T-002 in parallel** -- These are the simplest workflows (no GCP auth) and establish the pattern for workflow structure, naming, and step summaries.
   987→2. **Then T-003** -- Docker build is the most complex single workflow but only depends on self-contained GCP auth. The patterns from T-001/T-002 inform the overall structure.
   988→3. **Then T-004** -- Image promotion builds on the GCP auth pattern from T-003 and adds cross-repo PR complexity.
   989→4. **Finally T-005** -- End-to-end validation after all workflows are created.
   990→
   991→### Optimal Team Structure
   992→
   993→- **1 engineer** can handle all 5 tasks sequentially (estimated 2-3 days)
   994→- **2 engineers** can parallelize: Engineer A does T-001 + T-003, Engineer B does T-002 + T-004, both collaborate on T-005
   995→
   996→### Parallelization Opportunities
   997→
   998→- T-001 and T-002 are fully independent and can be worked on simultaneously
   999→- T-003 and T-004 share the GCP auth pattern but are otherwise independent; however, establishing the auth pattern in T-003 first reduces risk in T-004
  1000→
  1001→### Resource Allocation
  1002→
  1003→- All work is in the `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` repository
  1004→- No infrastructure changes required -- these are workflow files only
  1005→- Testing requires access to a consumer repo with the correct GitHub Environment variables configured (`vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER`, `vars.SERVICE_NAME`)
  1006→
  1007→## Critical Path Analysis
  1008→
  1009→### Tasks on Critical Path
  1010→
  1011→```
  1012→T-001/T-002 (parallel) --> T-003 --> T-004 --> T-005
  1013→```
  1014→
  1015→- **T-003 (docker-build-push.yml)** is the highest-risk item due to complexity (GCP auth, Docker build, Trivy, Cosign integration)
  1016→- **T-004 (image-promote.yml)** is the second-highest-risk due to cross-repo PR creation and GitHub App token requirements
  1017→
  1018→### Potential Bottlenecks
  1019→
  1020→1. **GCP OIDC auth block** -- Getting this right is critical. Must exactly mirror `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116. Any deviation will cause auth failures across all consuming repos.
  1021→2. **GitHub App configuration** -- T-004 requires a GitHub App to be configured with cross-repo permissions. If this does not exist yet, it blocks the PR creation portion of image promotion.
  1022→3. **`static-ip-runner` tool availability** -- The self-hosted runner must have `helm`, `jq`, `gcloud`, `cosign`, and `trivy` available. If any are missing, setup steps must be added.
  1023→
  1024→### Schedule Optimization
  1025→
  1026→- Start T-001 and T-002 on day 1 (both complete by end of day 1)
  1027→- Start T-003 on day 1 afternoon or day 2 morning (complete by end of day 2)
  1028→- Start T-004 on day 2 (complete by end of day 2 or early day 3)
  1029→- T-005 validation on day 3 (half day)
  1030→- **Total estimated duration**: 2.5-3 working days for 1 engineer
  1031→
  1032→---
  1033→
  1034→## Anti-Patterns to Avoid (from PRP)
  1035→
  1036→These are documented in the PRP and must be checked during T-005 validation:
  1037→
  1038→- Do NOT hardcode `europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images` -- construct from `vars` or inputs
  1039→- Do NOT skip Trivy scanning -- it is a security gate, not optional
  1040→- Do NOT use `ubuntu-latest` -- all runners must be `static-ip-runner`
  1041→- Do NOT duplicate the GCP auth block -- copy it exactly from `terraform-plan.yml` lines 82-116
  1042→- Do NOT commit directly to workload repos -- always create a PR for config changes
  1043→- Do NOT mix `secrets` and `vars` -- GCP credentials use `vars` (environment variables), not `secrets`
  1044→- Do NOT use `docker/login-action` for AR auth -- use `gcloud auth configure-docker` after OIDC auth
  1045→- Do NOT forget `--yes` flag on `cosign sign` -- required for non-interactive keyless signing
  1046→

    56→    steps:

    60→      - name: Setup Terraform
    61→        uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3
    62→        with:
    63→          terraform_version: ${{ inputs.terraform_version }}
    64→          terraform_wrapper: false
    65→
    66→      - name: Configure GCP Auth
    67→        id: gcp-auth-config
    68→        run: |

    72→
    73→          if [ -z "$PROJECT_NUMBER" ] || [ -z "$SERVICE_NAME" ]; then

    75→            echo "::error::Set GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER and SERVICE_NAME as GitHub Environment variables for '${{ inputs.environment }}'."
    76→            exit 1
    77→          fi
    78→
    79→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
    80→          POOL_ID="github-${SERVICE_NAME}-${ENV}"
    81→          PROVIDER="projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider"
    82→
    83→          # Allow explicit GKE project ID override (e.g., shared env uses shr abbreviation)
    84→          GKE_PROJECT="${{ vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID }}"
    85→          if [ -z "$GKE_PROJECT" ]; then
    86→            GKE_PROJECT="kd-ix-eur-${ENV}-gke"
    87→          fi

   100→
   101→      - name: Setup Cloud SDK
   102→        uses: google-github-actions/setup-gcloud@v2
   103→
   104→      - name: Generate GitHub App Token

   111→          owner: konecta-ix-platform
   112→
   113→      - name: Configure Git for Private Modules
   114→        if: steps.app-token.outcome == 'success'
