   116→          git config --global url."https://x-access-token:${{ steps.app-token.outputs.token }}@github.com/".insteadOf "https://github.com/"
   117→          echo "Configured git for private module access"
   118→
   119→      - name: Terraform Init
   120→        run: terraform init
   121→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   122→
   123→      - name: Pre-Apply Validation
   124→        run: terraform validate
   125→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   126→
   127→      - name: Fresh Plan (Pre-Apply)
   128→        id: pre-plan
   129→        run: |
   130→          echo "Generating fresh plan to catch any drift..."
   131→          terraform plan -out=tfplan -no-color 2>&1 | tee pre_plan_output.txt
   132→
   133→          if grep -q "No changes" pre_plan_output.txt; then
   134→            echo "has_changes=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   135→            echo "::warning::No changes detected in fresh plan. Deployment may be unnecessary."
   136→          else
   137→            echo "has_changes=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   138→          fi
   139→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   140→
   141→      - name: Terraform Apply
   142→        id: apply
   143→        if: steps.pre-plan.outputs.has_changes == 'true'
   144→        run: |
   145→          terraform apply -auto-approve tfplan 2>&1 | tee apply_output.txt
   146→          APPLY_EXIT_CODE=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
   147→          echo "exit_code=$APPLY_EXIT_CODE" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   148→          exit $APPLY_EXIT_CODE
   149→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   150→        continue-on-error: true
   151→
   152→      - name: Skip Apply (No Changes)
   153→        if: steps.pre-plan.outputs.has_changes == 'false'
   154→        run: |
   155→          echo "Skipping apply - no changes detected"
   156→          echo "exit_code=0" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   157→
   158→      - name: Capture Outputs
   159→        id: outputs
   160→        if: steps.apply.outputs.exit_code == '0' || steps.pre-plan.outputs.has_changes == 'false'
   161→        run: |
   162→          terraform output -json > outputs.json
   163→          {
   164→            echo 'json<<EOF'
   165→            cat outputs.json
   166→            echo EOF
   167→          } >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   168→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   169→
   170→      - name: Post-Apply Validation
   171→        if: steps.apply.outputs.exit_code == '0'
   172→        run: |
   173→          echo "Running post-apply validation..."
   174→          terraform plan -detailed-exitcode -no-color || {
   175→            EXIT_CODE=$?
   176→            if [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 2 ]; then
   177→              echo "::warning::Post-apply plan detected drift. This may indicate resource drift or provider issues."
   178→            fi
   179→          }
   180→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   181→        continue-on-error: true
   182→
   183→      - name: Deployment Timestamp
   184→        id: timestamp
   185→        run: |
   186→          echo "deployment_time=$(date -u +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   187→

   189→        id: artifact-name

   191→          SANITIZED=$(echo "${{ inputs.working_directory }}" | tr '/' '-')
   192→          echo "name=terraform-apply-${{ inputs.environment }}-${SANITIZED}-${{ github.run_id }}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   193→
   194→      - name: Upload Apply Output
   195→        if: always()
   196→        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
   197→        with:

   199→          path: |
   200→            ${{ inputs.working_directory }}/apply_output.txt
   201→            ${{ inputs.working_directory }}/outputs.json
   202→          retention-days: 90
   203→
   204→      - name: Apply Result
   205→        id: apply-result
   206→        if: always()

   208→          if [ "${{ steps.pre-plan.outputs.has_changes }}" == "false" ]; then
   209→            echo "status=success" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   210→            echo "No changes to apply"
   211→          elif [ "${{ steps.apply.outputs.exit_code }}" == "0" ]; then
   212→            echo "status=success" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   213→            echo "Apply completed successfully"
   214→          else
   215→            echo "status=failure" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   216→            echo "::error::Apply failed"
   217→            exit 1
   218→          fi
   219→
   220→      - name: Summary

   224→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   225→          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   226→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   227→          echo "**Deployment Time:** ${{ steps.timestamp.outputs.deployment_time }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   228→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   229→
   230→          if [ "${{ steps.pre-plan.outputs.has_changes }}" == "false" ]; then
   231→            echo "**Status:** No changes detected (skipped apply)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   232→          elif [ "${{ steps.apply.outputs.exit_code }}" == "0" ]; then
   233→            echo "**Status:** Applied successfully" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   234→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   235→            echo "<details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   236→            echo "<summary>Terraform Outputs</summary>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   237→            echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   238→            echo "\`\`\`json" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   239→            cat outputs.json >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   240→            echo "\`\`\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   241→            echo "</details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   242→          else
   243→            echo "**Status:** Apply failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   244→          fi
   245→        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
   246→

     1→name: z_ArgoCD Deploy (Reusable)
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  workflow_call:
     5→    inputs:
     6→      application_file:
     7→        description: 'Path to ArgoCD Application YAML'
     8→        required: true
     9→        type: string
    10→      argocd_version:
    11→        description: 'ArgoCD CLI version'
    12→        required: false
    13→        type: string
    14→        default: '2.13.3'
    15→      wait_for_sync:
    16→        description: 'Wait for sync + healthy'
    17→        required: false
    18→        type: boolean
    19→        default: true
    20→      sync_timeout:
    21→        description: 'Sync wait timeout (seconds)'
    22→        required: false
    23→        type: number
    24→        default: 300
    25→    secrets:
    26→      argocd_auth_token:
    27→        required: true
    28→    outputs:
    29→      app_name:
    30→        description: 'Deployed application name'
    31→        value: ${{ jobs.deploy.outputs.app_name }}
    32→      sync_status:
    33→        description: 'Final sync status'
    34→        value: ${{ jobs.deploy.outputs.sync_status }}
    35→
    36→permissions:
    37→  contents: read
    38→
    39→jobs:
    40→  deploy:
    41→    name: ArgoCD Deploy
    42→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    43→    outputs:
    44→      app_name: ${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}
    45→      sync_status: ${{ steps.result.outputs.sync_status }}
    46→
    47→    env:
    48→      ARGOCD_SERVER: argocd-ci.ix.konecta-digital.com
    49→      ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.argocd_auth_token }}
    50→      ARGOCD_OPTS: '--grpc-web'
    51→
    52→    steps:
    53→      - name: Checkout code
    54→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    55→
    56→      - name: Install ArgoCD CLI
    57→        run: |
    58→          VERSION="${{ inputs.argocd_version }}"
    59→          ARCH="$(uname -m)"
    60→          case "$ARCH" in
    61→            x86_64) ARCH="amd64" ;;
    62→            aarch64|arm64) ARCH="arm64" ;;
    63→            *) echo "::error::Unsupported architecture: $ARCH"; exit 1 ;;
    64→          esac
    65→
    66→          OS="$(uname -s | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
    67→          URL="https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/releases/download/v${VERSION}/argocd-${OS}-${ARCH}"
    68→
    69→          echo "Downloading ArgoCD CLI v${VERSION} (${OS}/${ARCH})..."
    70→          curl -sSL -o /usr/local/bin/argocd "$URL"
    71→          chmod +x /usr/local/bin/argocd
    72→          argocd version --client --short
    73→
    74→      - name: Verify ArgoCD connectivity
    75→        run: |
    76→          argocd app list --output name > /dev/null
    77→          echo "Authenticated to ArgoCD at $ARGOCD_SERVER"
    78→
    79→      - name: Validate application file
    80→        run: |
    81→          FILE="${{ inputs.application_file }}"
    82→          if [ ! -f "$FILE" ]; then
    83→            echo "::error::Application file not found: $FILE"
    84→            exit 1
    85→          fi
    86→          echo "Found application file: $FILE"
    87→
    88→      - name: Extract application name
    89→        id: extract-name
    90→        run: |
    91→          FILE="${{ inputs.application_file }}"
    92→          APP_NAME=$(grep -E '^\s+name:\s' "$FILE" | head -1 | sed 's/.*name:\s*//' | tr -d '[:space:]' | tr -d '"' | tr -d "'")
    93→          if [ -z "$APP_NAME" ]; then
    94→            echo "::error::Could not extract application name from $FILE"
    95→            exit 1
    96→          fi
    97→          echo "app_name=$APP_NAME" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
    98→          echo "Application name: $APP_NAME"
    99→
   100→      - name: Deploy application
   101→        run: |
   102→          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
   103→          FILE="${{ inputs.application_file }}"
   104→          echo "Deploying application '$APP_NAME' from $FILE..."
   105→          argocd app create -f "$FILE" --upsert
   106→          echo "Application '$APP_NAME' deployed (upsert)"
   107→
   108→      - name: Wait for in-flight operation
   109→        if: inputs.wait_for_sync
   110→        run: |
   111→          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
   112→          echo "Waiting for any in-flight operation on '$APP_NAME' to complete..."
   113→          argocd app wait "$APP_NAME" --operation --timeout ${{ inputs.sync_timeout }} || true
   114→          echo "No in-flight operation (or completed)"
   115→
   116→      - name: Sync application
   117→        if: inputs.wait_for_sync
   118→        run: |
   119→          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
   120→          echo "Syncing application '$APP_NAME'..."
   121→          argocd app sync "$APP_NAME"
   122→
   123→      - name: Wait for healthy
   124→        if: inputs.wait_for_sync
   125→        run: |
   126→          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
   127→          TIMEOUT="${{ inputs.sync_timeout }}"
   128→          echo "Waiting for '$APP_NAME' to be healthy (timeout: ${TIMEOUT}s)..."
   129→          argocd app wait "$APP_NAME" --health --timeout "$TIMEOUT"
   130→
   131→      - name: Deployment result
   132→        id: result
   133→        if: always()
   134→        run: |
   135→          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
   136→          if [ -z "$APP_NAME" ]; then
   137→            echo "sync_status=unknown" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   138→            echo "::error::Could not determine application name"
   139→            exit 1
   140→          fi
   141→
   142→          STATUS=$(argocd app get "$APP_NAME" -o json 2>/dev/null | grep -o '"sync":{"status":"[^"]*"' | head -1 | sed 's/.*"status":"//' | tr -d '"' || echo "unknown")
   143→          HEALTH=$(argocd app get "$APP_NAME" -o json 2>/dev/null | grep -o '"health":{"status":"[^"]*"' | head -1 | sed 's/.*"status":"//' | tr -d '"' || echo "unknown")
   144→          echo "sync_status=${STATUS}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   145→
   146→          echo "## ArgoCD Deployment Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   147→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   148→          echo "**Application:** \`$APP_NAME\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   149→          echo "**Server:** $ARGOCD_SERVER" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   150→          echo "**Sync Status:** $STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   151→          echo "**Health Status:** $HEALTH" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   152→          echo "**Source:** \`${{ inputs.application_file }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   153→          echo "**Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   154→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   155→
   156→          if [ "$HEALTH" == "Healthy" ] && [ "$STATUS" == "Synced" ]; then
   157→            echo "**Result:** Deployment successful" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   158→          else
   159→            echo "**Result:** Deployment may need attention (Health: $HEALTH, Sync: $STATUS)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   160→          fi
   161→

{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-lint.yml",
  "content": "name: z_Helm Lint (Reusable)\n\n# Reusable workflow for Helm chart linting and template validation\n# Supports multiple values files and strict mode\non:\n  workflow_call:\n    inputs:\n      chart_path:\n        description: 'Path to Helm chart directory'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      values_files:\n        description: 'Comma-separated list of values files (relative to chart_path)'\n        required: false\n        type: string\n        default: 'values.yaml'\n      strict:\n        description: 'Enable strict linting mode'\n        required: false\n        type: boolean\n        default: true\n      environment:\n        description: 'Environment label for summary display'\n        required: false\n        type: string\n        default: 'dev'\n    outputs:\n      lint_status:\n        description: 'Lint result (success or failure)'\n        value: ${{ jobs.lint.outputs.lint_status }}\n      template_status:\n        description: 'Template render result (success or failure)'\n        value: ${{ jobs.lint.outputs.template_status }}\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  lint:\n    name: Helm Lint\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    outputs:\n      lint_status: ${{ steps.result.outputs.lint_status }}\n      template_status: ${{ steps.result.outputs.template_status }}\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout code\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - name: Setup Helm\n        uses: azure/setup-helm@v4\n\n      - name: Build values file flags\n        id: values-flags\n        run: |\n          CHART_PATH=\"${{ inputs.chart_path }}\"\n          VALUES_INPUT=\"${{ inputs.values_files }}\"\n          FLAGS=\"\"\n\n          IFS=',' read -ra FILES <<< \"$VALUES_INPUT\"\n          for file in \"${FILES[@]}\"; do\n            file=$(echo \"$file\" | xargs)  # trim whitespace\n            if [ -n \"$file\" ]; then\n              FULL_PATH=\"${CHART_PATH}/${file}\"\n              if [ -f \"$FULL_PATH\" ]; then\n                FLAGS=\"$FLAGS -f $FULL_PATH\"\n              else\n                echo \"::warning::Values file not found: $FULL_PATH\"\n              fi\n            fi\n          done\n\n          echo \"flags=${FLAGS}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"Using values flags: ${FLAGS}\"\n\n      - name: Helm Lint\n        id: lint\n        run: |\n          CHART_PATH=\"${{ inputs.chart_path }}\"\n          STRICT_FLAG=\"\"\n          if [ \"${{ inputs.strict }}\" == \"true\" ]; then\n            STRICT_FLAG=\"--strict\"\n          fi\n\n          echo \"Running: helm lint ${CHART_PATH} ${{ steps.values-flags.outputs.flags }} ${STRICT_FLAG}\"\n          helm lint \"${CHART_PATH}\" ${{ steps.values-flags.outputs.flags }} ${STRICT_FLAG} 2>&1 | tee lint_output.txt\n          EXIT_CODE=${PIPESTATUS[0]}\n          echo \"exit_code=${EXIT_CODE}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          exit $EXIT_CODE\n        continue-on-error: true\n\n      - name: Helm Template\n        id: template\n        run: |\n          CHART_PATH=\"${{ inputs.chart_path }}\"\n\n          echo \"Running: helm template test-release ${CHART_PATH} ${{ steps.values-flags.outputs.flags }}\"\n          helm template test-release \"${CHART_PATH}\" ${{ steps.values-flags.outputs.flags }} > /dev/null 2>&1\n          EXIT_CODE=$?\n          echo \"exit_code=${EXIT_CODE}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n\n          if [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ]; then\n            echo \"Template render failed. Full output:\"\n            helm template test-release \"${CHART_PATH}\" ${{ steps.values-flags.outputs.flags }} 2>&1 | tee template_output.txt || true\n          fi\n          exit $EXIT_CODE\n        continue-on-error: true\n\n      - name: Lint and Template Result\n        id: result\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ steps.lint.outcome }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"lint_status=success\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          else\n            echo \"lint_status=failure\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${{ steps.template.outcome }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"template_status=success\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          else\n            echo \"template_status=failure\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          fi\n\n      - name: Summary\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          echo \"## [${{ inputs.environment }}] Helm Lint Results\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Chart:** \\`${{ inputs.chart_path }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Values:** \\`${{ inputs.values_files }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Strict Mode:** ${{ inputs.strict }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"| Check | Status |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"|-------|--------|\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          if [ \"${{ steps.lint.outcome }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"| Lint | Passed |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| Lint | **FAILED** |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${{ steps.template.outcome }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"| Template Render | Passed |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| Template Render | **FAILED** |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ -f lint_output.txt ]; then\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"<details>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"<summary>Lint Output</summary>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\\`\\`\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            cat lint_output.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\\`\\`\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"</details>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n      - name: Fail if checks did not pass\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ steps.lint.outcome }}\" == \"failure\" ] || [ \"${{ steps.template.outcome }}\" == \"failure\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Helm validation failed\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/helm-values-check.yml",
  "content": "name: z_Helm Values Check (Reusable)\n\n# Reusable workflow to validate that committed Helm values files\n# match what generate-helm-values.sh would produce from config.json\non:\n  workflow_call:\n    inputs:\n      environment:\n        description: 'Environment name (dev, qa, prod)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      script_path:\n        description: 'Path to the Helm values generation script'\n        required: false\n        type: string\n        default: './scripts/generate-helm-values.sh'\n    outputs:\n      check_status:\n        description: 'Check result (success or failure)'\n        value: ${{ jobs.check.outputs.check_status }}\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  check:\n    name: Helm Values Check\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    outputs:\n      check_status: ${{ steps.result.outputs.check_status }}\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout code\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - name: Verify jq is available\n        run: |\n          if ! command -v jq &> /dev/null; then\n            echo \"::error::jq is required but not found on this runner\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          echo \"jq version: $(jq --version)\"\n\n      - name: Verify script exists\n        run: |\n          SCRIPT=\"${{ inputs.script_path }}\"\n          if [ ! -f \"$SCRIPT\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Values generation script not found: $SCRIPT\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          echo \"Found script: $SCRIPT\"\n\n      - name: Run values check\n        id: check\n        run: |\n          SCRIPT=\"${{ inputs.script_path }}\"\n          ENV=\"${{ inputs.environment }}\"\n\n          echo \"Running: bash ${SCRIPT} ${ENV} --check\"\n          bash \"${SCRIPT}\" \"${ENV}\" --check 2>&1 | tee check_output.txt\n          EXIT_CODE=${PIPESTATUS[0]}\n          echo \"exit_code=${EXIT_CODE}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          exit $EXIT_CODE\n        continue-on-error: true\n\n      - name: Check Result\n        id: result\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ steps.check.outcome }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"check_status=success\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          else\n            echo \"check_status=failure\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          fi\n\n      - name: Summary\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          echo \"## [${{ inputs.environment }}] Helm Values Check\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Script:** \\`${{ inputs.script_path }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"| Check | Status |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"|-------|--------|\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          if [ \"${{ steps.check.outcome }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"| Values Drift | No drift detected |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| Values Drift | **DRIFT DETECTED** |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ -f check_output.txt ]; then\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"<details>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"<summary>Check Output</summary>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\\`\\`\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            cat check_output.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\\`\\`\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"</details>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n      - name: Fail if drift detected\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ steps.check.outcome }}\" == \"failure\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Helm values drift detected for ${{ inputs.environment }}. Run '${{ inputs.script_path }} ${{ inputs.environment }}' to regenerate.\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/docker-build-push.yml",
  "content": "name: z_Docker Build Push (Reusable)\n\n# Reusable workflow for Docker image build, scan, sign, and push to Artifact Registry\n# Handles GCP OIDC authentication self-contained via environment variables\non:\n  workflow_call:\n    inputs:\n      image_name:\n        description: 'Docker image name (e.g., kix-app-iqportal)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      dockerfile_path:\n        description: 'Path to Dockerfile'\n        required: false\n        type: string\n        default: './Dockerfile'\n      build_context:\n        description: 'Docker build context path'\n        required: false\n        type: string\n        default: '.'\n      environment:\n        description: 'Environment name (dev, qa, prod)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      push:\n        description: 'Push image to registry'\n        required: false\n        type: boolean\n        default: true\n      enable_trivy:\n        description: 'Enable Trivy vulnerability scanning'\n        required: false\n        type: boolean\n        default: true\n      trivy_severity:\n        description: 'Trivy severity threshold (e.g., CRITICAL,HIGH)'\n        required: false\n        type: string\n        default: 'CRITICAL,HIGH'\n      enable_cosign:\n        description: 'Enable Cosign keyless image signing'\n        required: false\n        type: boolean\n        default: true\n      build_args:\n        description: 'Docker build args (newline-separated KEY=VALUE)'\n        required: false\n        type: string\n        default: ''\n    outputs:\n      image_uri:\n        description: 'Full image URI with digest'\n        value: ${{ jobs.build-push.outputs.image_uri }}\n      image_tag:\n        description: 'Image tag (git SHA)'\n        value: ${{ jobs.build-push.outputs.image_tag }}\n      trivy_findings_count:\n        description: 'Total Trivy findings (CRITICAL + HIGH)'\n        value: ${{ jobs.build-push.outputs.trivy_findings_count }}\n      trivy_critical_count:\n        description: 'CRITICAL severity findings'\n        value: ${{ jobs.build-push.outputs.trivy_critical_count }}\n      trivy_high_count:\n        description: 'HIGH severity findings'\n        value: ${{ jobs.build-push.outputs.trivy_high_count }}\n      scan_passed:\n        description: 'Whether security scan passed (true/false)'\n        value: ${{ jobs.build-push.outputs.scan_passed }}\n\npermissions:\n  id-token: write\n  contents: read\n  security-events: write\n  packages: write\n\njobs:\n  build-push:\n    name: Docker Build Push\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}\n    outputs:\n      image_uri: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_uri }}\n      image_tag: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_tag }}\n      trivy_findings_count: ${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.findings_count }}\n      trivy_critical_count: ${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.critical_count }}\n      trivy_high_count: ${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.high_count }}\n      scan_passed: ${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.scan_passed }}\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout code\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - name: Configure GCP Auth\n        id: gcp-auth-config\n        run: |\n          # Read from calling repo's environment variables\n          PROJECT_NUMBER=\"${{ vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER }}\"\n          SERVICE_NAME=\"${{ vars.SERVICE_NAME }}\"\n\n          if [ -z \"$PROJECT_NUMBER\" ] || [ -z \"$SERVICE_NAME\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Missing required environment variables.\"\n            echo \"::error::Set GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER and SERVICE_NAME as GitHub Environment variables for '${{ inputs.environment }}'.\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          ENV=\"${{ inputs.environment }}\"\n          POOL_ID=\"github-${SERVICE_NAME}-${ENV}\"\n          PROVIDER=\"projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider\"\n\n          # Allow explicit GKE project ID override (e.g., shared env uses shr abbreviation)\n          GKE_PROJECT=\"${{ vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID }}\"\n          if [ -z \"$GKE_PROJECT\" ]; then\n            GKE_PROJECT=\"kd-ix-eur-${ENV}-gke\"\n          fi\n          SA=\"${POOL_ID}@${GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com\"\n\n          echo \"provider_id=${PROVIDER}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"service_account=${SA}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"Using WIF pool: ${POOL_ID} (project ${PROJECT_NUMBER})\"\n          echo \"Service account: ${SA}\"\n\n      - name: Authenticate to GCP\n        uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2\n        with:\n          workload_identity_provider: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.provider_id }}\n          service_account: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.service_account }}\n\n      - name: Setup Cloud SDK\n        uses: google-github-actions/setup-gcloud@v2\n\n      - name: Configure Docker for Artifact Registry\n        run: gcloud auth configure-docker europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev --quiet\n\n      - name: Setup Docker Buildx\n        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3\n\n      - name: Generate image metadata\n        id: meta\n        run: |\n          IMAGE_REPO=\"europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}\"\n          TAG=\"${{ github.sha }}\"\n          FULL_IMAGE=\"${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG}\"\n\n          echo \"image_repo=${IMAGE_REPO}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"image_tag=${TAG}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"image_uri=${FULL_IMAGE}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"tags=${IMAGE_REPO}:${TAG},${IMAGE_REPO}:latest\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"Image: ${FULL_IMAGE}\"\n\n      - name: Build and Push\n        id: build\n        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6\n        with:\n          context: ${{ inputs.build_context }}\n          file: ${{ inputs.dockerfile_path }}\n          push: ${{ inputs.push }}\n          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}\n          cache-from: type=gha\n          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max\n          provenance: true\n          sbom: true\n          build-args: ${{ inputs.build_args }}\n\n      - name: Run Trivy Security Scan (SARIF)\n        if: inputs.enable_trivy && inputs.push\n        uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1\n        with:\n          scan-type: 'image'\n          image-ref: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_uri }}\n          format: 'sarif'\n          output: 'trivy-results.sarif'\n          severity: 'LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH,CRITICAL'\n          exit-code: '0'\n\n      - name: Run Trivy Security Scan (Table)\n        if: inputs.enable_trivy && inputs.push\n        uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.33.1\n        continue-on-error: true\n        with:\n          scan-type: 'image'\n          image-ref: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_uri }}\n          format: 'table'\n          output: 'trivy-results.txt'\n          severity: 'LOW,MEDIUM,HIGH,CRITICAL'\n          exit-code: '0'\n\n      - name: Upload Trivy SARIF\n        if: inputs.enable_trivy && inputs.push && always()\n        continue-on-error: true\n        uses: github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v3\n        with:\n          sarif_file: trivy-results.sarif\n          category: trivy-${{ inputs.image_name }}\n\n      - name: Upload Trivy Results as Artifact\n        if: inputs.enable_trivy && inputs.push && always()\n        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4\n        with:\n          name: trivy-results-${{ inputs.image_name }}-${{ github.sha }}\n          path: |\n            trivy-results.sarif\n            trivy-results.txt\n          retention-days: 30\n\n      - name: Count Trivy findings by severity\n        id: trivy-counts\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ inputs.enable_trivy }}\" == \"true\" ] && [ -f trivy-results.txt ]; then\n            CRITICAL=$(grep -oP 'CRITICAL:\\s*\\K\\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')\n            HIGH=$(grep -oP 'HIGH:\\s*\\K\\d+' trivy-results.txt | awk '{s+=$1} END {print s+0}')\n            TOTAL=$((CRITICAL + HIGH))\n\n            echo \"findings_count=$TOTAL\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"critical_count=$CRITICAL\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"high_count=$HIGH\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n\n            if [ \"$CRITICAL\" -gt 0 ]; then\n              echo \"scan_passed=false\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n              echo \"::warning::Found $CRITICAL CRITICAL and $HIGH HIGH severity vulnerabilities\"\n            else\n              echo \"scan_passed=true\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            fi\n          else\n            echo \"findings_count=0\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"critical_count=0\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"high_count=0\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"scan_passed=true\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          fi\n\n      - name: Setup Cosign\n        if: inputs.enable_cosign && inputs.push\n        uses: sigstore/cosign-installer@v3\n\n      - name: Sign image with Cosign\n        if: inputs.enable_cosign && inputs.push && steps.trivy-counts.outputs.scan_passed == 'true'\n        run: |\n          DIGEST=\"${{ steps.build.outputs.digest }}\"\n          IMAGE_REPO=\"${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_repo }}\"\n          echo \"Signing ${IMAGE_REPO}@${DIGEST}...\"\n          cosign sign --yes \"${IMAGE_REPO}@${DIGEST}\"\n          echo \"Image signed successfully\"\n\n      - name: Summary\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          echo \"## [${{ inputs.environment }}] Docker Build Push\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Image:** \\`${{ steps.meta.outputs.image_uri }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Digest:** \\`${{ steps.build.outputs.digest || 'N/A' }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Push:** ${{ inputs.push }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"| Step | Status |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"|------|--------|\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          if [ \"${{ steps.build.outcome }}\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"| Build | Passed |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| Build | **FAILED** |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${{ inputs.enable_trivy }}\" == \"true\" ]; then\n            CRITICAL=\"${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.critical_count }}\"\n            HIGH=\"${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.high_count }}\"\n            SCAN_PASSED=\"${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.scan_passed }}\"\n            if [ \"$SCAN_PASSED\" == \"true\" ]; then\n              echo \"| Security Scan | Passed (${CRITICAL} critical, ${HIGH} high) |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            else\n              echo \"| Security Scan | **FAILED** (${CRITICAL} critical, ${HIGH} high) |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            fi\n          else\n            echo \"| Security Scan | Skipped |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${{ inputs.enable_cosign }}\" == \"true\" ] && [ \"${{ inputs.push }}\" == \"true\" ]; then\n            if [ \"${{ steps.trivy-counts.outputs.scan_passed }}\" == \"true\" ]; then\n              echo \"| Image Signing | Signed |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            else\n              echo \"| Image Signing | Skipped (scan failed) |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            fi\n          else\n            echo \"| Image Signing | Skipped |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${{ inputs.enable_trivy }}\" == \"true\" ] && [ -f trivy-results.txt ]; then\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"### Vulnerability Summary\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"<details>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"<summary>Trivy Scan Results</summary>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\\`\\`\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            head -100 trivy-results.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"\\`\\`\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"</details>\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/image-promote.yml",
  "content": "name: z_Image Promote (Reusable)\n\n# Reusable workflow for promoting container images across environments\n# Re-tags image in Artifact Registry and creates PR to update config.json in workload repo\non:\n  workflow_call:\n    inputs:\n      image_name:\n        description: 'Docker image name (e.g., kix-app-iqportal)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      source_tag:\n        description: 'Source image tag to promote (e.g., git SHA from dev build)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      target_environment:\n        description: 'Target environment (qa, prod)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      workload_repo:\n        description: 'Workload repo (e.g., konecta-ix-workloads/kix-workload-konectaiq)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      config_path:\n        description: 'Path to config.json in workload repo (e.g., environments/qa/config.json)'\n        required: true\n        type: string\n      require_approval:\n        description: 'Require GitHub environment approval before promotion'\n        required: false\n        type: boolean\n        default: true\n    secrets:\n      WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID:\n        description: 'GitHub App ID for cross-repo workload access'\n        required: true\n      WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY:\n        description: 'GitHub App private key for cross-repo workload access'\n        required: true\n    outputs:\n      promoted_image_uri:\n        description: 'Full image URI with environment tag'\n        value: ${{ jobs.promote.outputs.promoted_image_uri }}\n      pr_url:\n        description: 'URL of created PR in workload repo'\n        value: ${{ jobs.promote.outputs.pr_url }}\n      pr_number:\n        description: 'PR number in workload repo'\n        value: ${{ jobs.promote.outputs.pr_number }}\n\npermissions:\n  id-token: write\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  promote:\n    name: Image Promote\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    environment: ${{ inputs.require_approval && inputs.target_environment || '' }}\n    outputs:\n      promoted_image_uri: ${{ steps.retag.outputs.promoted_image_uri }}\n      pr_url: ${{ steps.create-pr.outputs.pr_url }}\n      pr_number: ${{ steps.create-pr.outputs.pr_number }}\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Configure GCP Auth\n        id: gcp-auth-config\n        run: |\n          # Read from calling repo's environment variables\n          PROJECT_NUMBER=\"${{ vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER }}\"\n          SERVICE_NAME=\"${{ vars.SERVICE_NAME }}\"\n\n          if [ -z \"$PROJECT_NUMBER\" ] || [ -z \"$SERVICE_NAME\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Missing required environment variables.\"\n            echo \"::error::Set GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER and SERVICE_NAME as GitHub Environment variables for '${{ inputs.target_environment }}'.\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          ENV=\"${{ inputs.target_environment }}\"\n          POOL_ID=\"github-${SERVICE_NAME}-${ENV}\"\n          PROVIDER=\"projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider\"\n\n          # Allow explicit GKE project ID override (e.g., shared env uses shr abbreviation)\n          GKE_PROJECT=\"${{ vars.GCP_GKE_PROJECT_ID }}\"\n          if [ -z \"$GKE_PROJECT\" ]; then\n            GKE_PROJECT=\"kd-ix-eur-${ENV}-gke\"\n          fi\n          SA=\"${POOL_ID}@${GKE_PROJECT}.iam.gserviceaccount.com\"\n\n          echo \"provider_id=${PROVIDER}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"service_account=${SA}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"Using WIF pool: ${POOL_ID} (project ${PROJECT_NUMBER})\"\n          echo \"Service account: ${SA}\"\n\n      - name: Authenticate to GCP\n        uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2\n        with:\n          workload_identity_provider: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.provider_id }}\n          service_account: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.service_account }}\n\n      - name: Setup Cloud SDK\n        uses: google-github-actions/setup-gcloud@v2\n\n      - name: Verify source image exists\n        run: |\n          IMAGE=\"europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}:${{ inputs.source_tag }}\"\n          echo \"Verifying source image: ${IMAGE}\"\n          gcloud artifacts docker images describe \"${IMAGE}\" --format=\"value(image_summary.digest)\"\n          echo \"Source image verified\"\n\n      - name: Add target environment tag\n        id: retag\n        run: |\n          SOURCE=\"europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}:${{ inputs.source_tag }}\"\n          TARGET=\"europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}:${{ inputs.target_environment }}-${{ inputs.source_tag }}\"\n\n          echo \"Re-tagging: ${SOURCE} -> ${TARGET}\"\n          gcloud artifacts docker tags add \"${SOURCE}\" \"${TARGET}\"\n          echo \"promoted_image_uri=${TARGET}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"Image re-tagged successfully\"\n\n      - name: Generate GitHub App Token\n        id: app-token\n        uses: actions/create-github-app-token@v1\n        with:\n          app-id: ${{ secrets.WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_ID }}\n          private-key: ${{ secrets.WORKLOAD_REPO_APP_PRIVATE_KEY }}\n          owner: ${{ github.repository_owner }}\n          repositories: ${{ github.event.repository.name }}\n\n      - name: Clone workload repo\n        run: |\n          REPO=\"${{ inputs.workload_repo }}\"\n          TOKEN=\"${{ steps.app-token.outputs.token }}\"\n          echo \"Cloning ${REPO}...\"\n          git clone \"https://x-access-token:${TOKEN}@github.com/${REPO}.git\" workload-repo\n          cd workload-repo\n          git config user.name \"github-actions[bot]\"\n          git config user.email \"github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com\"\n\n      - name: Update config.json image tag\n        run: |\n          cd workload-repo\n          CONFIG=\"${{ inputs.config_path }}\"\n\n          echo \"Updating ${CONFIG} with image tag: ${{ inputs.source_tag }}\"\n          jq --arg tag \"${{ inputs.source_tag }}\" '.service.image.tag = $tag' \"${CONFIG}\" > tmp.json && mv tmp.json \"${CONFIG}\"\n\n          echo \"Updated config.json:\"\n          jq '.service.image' \"${CONFIG}\"\n\n      - name: Regenerate Helm values\n        run: |\n          cd workload-repo\n          if [ -f scripts/generate-helm-values.sh ]; then\n            echo \"Regenerating Helm values for ${{ inputs.target_environment }}...\"\n            bash scripts/generate-helm-values.sh \"${{ inputs.target_environment }}\"\n            echo \"Helm values regenerated\"\n          else\n            echo \"No generate-helm-values.sh found, skipping values regeneration\"\n          fi\n\n      - name: Create promotion PR\n        id: create-pr\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ steps.app-token.outputs.token }}\n        run: |\n          cd workload-repo\n          BRANCH=\"promote/${{ inputs.image_name }}/${{ inputs.target_environment }}/${{ inputs.source_tag }}\"\n          TITLE=\"promote: ${{ inputs.image_name }} to ${{ inputs.target_environment }} (${{ inputs.source_tag }})\"\n\n          # Check if there are changes to commit\n          if git diff --quiet; then\n            echo \"::warning::No changes detected in workload repo. Image tag may already be set.\"\n            echo \"pr_url=\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            echo \"pr_number=\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          git checkout -b \"${BRANCH}\"\n          git add -A\n          git commit -m \"$(cat <<EOF\n          promote: ${{ inputs.image_name }} to ${{ inputs.target_environment }}\n\n          Image tag: ${{ inputs.source_tag }}\n          Source: ${{ github.server_url }}/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}\n          EOF\n          )\"\n\n          git push origin \"${BRANCH}\"\n\n          PR_URL=$(gh pr create \\\n            --repo \"${{ inputs.workload_repo }}\" \\\n            --title \"${TITLE}\" \\\n            --body \"$(cat <<EOF\n          ## Image Promotion\n\n          | Field | Value |\n          |-------|-------|\n          | **Image** | \\`${{ inputs.image_name }}\\` |\n          | **Tag** | \\`${{ inputs.source_tag }}\\` |\n          | **Target Environment** | ${{ inputs.target_environment }} |\n          | **Config Path** | \\`${{ inputs.config_path }}\\` |\n          | **Source Run** | [${{ github.repository }}#${{ github.run_number }}](${{ github.server_url }}/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${{ github.run_id }}) |\n          | **Promoted At** | $(date -u +\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\") |\n\n          ### Changes\n          - Updated \\`${{ inputs.config_path }}\\` image tag to \\`${{ inputs.source_tag }}\\`\n          - Regenerated Helm values from config.json (if applicable)\n\n          ### AR Image\n          \\`\\`\\`\n          europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts/approved-images/${{ inputs.image_name }}:${{ inputs.target_environment }}-${{ inputs.source_tag }}\n          \\`\\`\\`\n          EOF\n          )\" \\\n            --head \"${BRANCH}\" \\\n            --base main)\n\n          echo \"pr_url=${PR_URL}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          PR_NUMBER=$(echo \"${PR_URL}\" | grep -oP '/pull/\\K\\d+')\n          echo \"pr_number=${PR_NUMBER}\" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT\n          echo \"PR created: ${PR_URL}\"\n\n      - name: Summary\n        if: always()\n        run: |\n          echo \"## Image Promotion Summary\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Image:** \\`${{ inputs.image_name }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Source Tag:** \\`${{ inputs.source_tag }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Target Environment:** ${{ inputs.target_environment }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Workload Repo:** \\`${{ inputs.workload_repo }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Approval Required:** ${{ inputs.require_approval }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"| Step | Status |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"|------|--------|\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"| AR Re-tag | ${{ steps.retag.outputs.promoted_image_uri && 'Completed' || 'Failed' }} |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          PR_URL=\"${{ steps.create-pr.outputs.pr_url }}\"\n          if [ -n \"$PR_URL\" ]; then\n            echo \"| Config PR | [#${{ steps.create-pr.outputs.pr_number }}](${PR_URL}) |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| Config PR | No changes needed |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          if [ -n \"$PR_URL\" ]; then\n            echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"**Promoted Image:** \\`${{ steps.retag.outputs.promoted_image_uri }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n            echo \"**PR:** ${PR_URL}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n"
}

> AGENT

The push failed because the current OAuth token doesn't have the `workflow` scope, which is required to push files under `.github/workflows/`. This is a GitHub restriction — pushing workflow files requires the `workflow` scope on the token.

To fix this, you'll need to either:

1. **Re-authenticate with the `workflow` scope**:
   ```
   gh auth login -s workflow
   ```

2. **Or use SSH instead of HTTPS** (if you have an SSH key configured with write access).
